\"/content/drive/MyDrive/Phase5Bot/phase5_data.json\"]\n    for path in candidates:\n        if not os.path.exists(path):\n            continue\n        try:\n            with open(path, \"r\") as storage_state[\"last_restore\"] = now\n            storage_state[\"backup_available\"] = os.path.exists(DRIVE_BACKUP)\n            print(f\"[{PROJECT_NAME}] Loaded from Drive: {path}\")\n            return True\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"[{PROJECT_NAME}] Could not read Drive file {path}: {e}\")\n            storage_state[\"storage_errors\"] += 1\n\n    storage_state[\"drive_ok\"] = False\n    return False\n\n\n# \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n# UNIFIED save_data() / load_data()\n# All 13 existing call sites stay unchanged.\n# \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n\ndef save_data():\n    \"\"\"\n    Always writes local.  Also writes Drive if enabled.\n    Called by: buy, sell, auto-sell, stats update, portfolio update, reset.\n    \"\"\"\n    save_data_local()\n    save_data_drive()\n\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"\n    4-tier recovery sequence:\n      Drive primary \u2192 Drive backup \u2192 Local primary \u2192 Local backup\n    First valid file wins.  Backward compatible with all older save names.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Tier 1 & 2: Drive (primary then backup handled inside load_data_drive)\n    if storage_state[\"drive_enabled\"] and load_data_drive():\n        save_data_local()       # mirror to local immediately\n        return\n\n    # Tier 3 & 4: Local (primary then backup handled inside load_data_local)\n    if load_data_local():\n        save_data_drive()       # push to Drive if possible\n        return\n\n    print(f\"[{PROJECT_NAME}] Starting with clean state\")\n\n\n# \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n# PERIODIC SYNC  (called from background_loop)\n# \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n\ndef _maybe_periodic_sync():\n    \"\"\"\n    Save to Drive if DRIVE_SYNC_INTERVAL seconds have elapsed since last sync.\n    Designed to be called every background_loop tick (every 30 s).\n    \"\"\"\n    if not storage_state[\"drive_enabled\"]:\n        return\n    if time.time() - _last_drive_sync_epoch >= DRIVE_SYNC_INTERVAL:\n        print(f\"[{PROJECT_NAME}] Periodic Drive sync triggered\")\n        save_data_drive()\n\n\n# \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n# GRACEFUL SHUTDOWN SAVE\n# shutdown_save():\n    \"\"\"\n    Best-effort save called before Colab disconnect / manual stop.\n    Tries local first, then Drive, logs outcome.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"[{PROJECT_NAME}] Graceful shutdown save...\")\n    local_ok = save_data_local()\n    drive_ok = save_data_drive()\n    print(f\"[{PROJECT_NAME}] Shutdown save \u2014 Local: {local_ok}  Drive: {drive_ok}\")\n\n\n# \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n# STORAGE HEALTH DICT\n# \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n\ndef storage_health():\n    \"\"\"Return a dict describing the full current storage state.\"\"\"\n    local_size  = os.path.getsize(LOCAL_FILE)  if os.path.exists(LOCAL_FILE)  else 0\n    drive_size  = os.path.getsize(DRIVE_FILE)  if os.path.exists(DRIVE_FILE)  else 0\n    backup_avail = (\n        os.path.exists(LOCAL_BACKUP) or\n        os.path.exists(DRIVE_BACKUP) or\n        storage_state[\"backup_available\"]\n    )\n\n    return {\n        \"local_ok\":         storage_state[\"local_ok\"],\n        \"drive_ok\":         storage_state[\"drive_ok\"],\n        \"drive_enabled\":    storage_state[\"drive_enabled\"],\n        \"last_save\":        storage_state[\"last_save\"]       or \"never\",\n        \"last_drive_sync\":  storage_state[\"last_drive_sync\"] or \"never\",\n        \"last_restore\":     storage_state[\"last_restore\"]    or \"never\",\n        \"save_count\":       storage_state[\"save_count\"],\n        \"drive_sync_count\": storage_state[\"drive_sync_count\"],\n        \"storage_errors\":   storage_state[\"storage_errors\"],\n        \"backup_available\": backup_avail,\n        \"local_size\":       local_size,\n        \"drive_size\":       drive_size,\n        \"positions\":        len(positions),\n        \"trade_records\":    len(trade_log),\n    }\n\n\n# \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n# init_storage():\n    \"\"\"\n    Called once at bot startup.\n    Mounts Drive \u2192 creates project folder \u2192 loads data via 4-tier recovery.\n    \"\"\"\n    global storage_state\n\n    mounted = _mount_drive()\n    if mounted and _ensure_drive_dir():\n        storage_state[\"drive_enabled\"] = True\n        print(f\"[{PROJECT_NAME}] Google Drive enabled \u2705  ({DRIVE_DIR})\")\n    else:\n        storage_state[\"drive_enabled\"] = False\n        print(f\"[{PROJECT_NAME}] Running with local storage only\")\n\n    load_data()\n    print(\n        f\"[{PROJECT_NAME}] Ready | \"\n        f\"Drive: {storage_state['drive_enabled']} | \"\n        f\"Positions: {len(positions)} | \"\n        f\"Trades: {len(trade_log)}\"\n    )\n\n\n#========================================\n# GET LIVE PRICE\n# INR \u2192 USDT fallback\n#========================================\n\nimport requests\n\ndef get_price(coin):\n\n    try:\n\n        url = (\n            \"https://api.coindcx.com/exchange/ticker\"\n        )\n\n        response = requests.get(\n            url,\n            timeout=10\n        )\n\n        data = response.json()\n\n\n        # Try INR first\n\n        inr_pair = (\n\n            coin.upper()\n            +\n            \"INR\"\n\n        )\n\n\n        for item in data:\n\n            if item[\"market\"] == inr_pair:\n\n                price = float(\n\n                    item[\"last_price\"]\n\n                )\n\n                print(\n\n                    f\"{coin}: INR pair used\"\n\n                )\n\n                return price\n\n\n        # Fallback to USDT\n\n        usdt_pair = (\n\n            coin.upper()\n            +\n            \"USDT\"\n\n        )\n\n\n        usdt_price = None\n\n\n        for item in data:\n\n            if item[\"market\"] == usdt_pair:\n\n                usdt_price = float(\n\n                    item[\"last_price\"]\n\n                )\n\n                break\n\n\n        if usdt_price is None:\n\n            return 0\n\n\n        # Convert USDT \u2192 INR\n\n        usd_inr = 86\n\n\n        price_inr = (\n\n            usdt_price\n            *\n            usd_inr\n\n        )\n\n\n        print(\n\n            f\"{coin}: USDT fallback used\"\n\n        )\n\n\n        return round(\n\n            price_inr,\n            2\n\n        )\n\n\n    except Exception as e:\n\n        print(\n\n            f\"Price Error {coin}: {e}\"\n\n        )\n\n        return 0\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n#=========================================\n# GET BALANCES FUNCTION\n# =========================================\n\ndef get_balances():\n\n    url = \"https://api.coindcx.com/exchange/v1/users/balances\"\n\n    body = {\n        \"timestamp\": int(time.time() * 1000)\n    }\n\n    payload = json.dumps(body, separators=(',', ':'))\n\n    signature = hmac.new(\n        SECRET_KEY.encode(),\n        payload.encode(),\n        hashlib.sha256\n    ).hexdigest()\n\n    headers = {\n        \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\",\n        \"X-AUTH-APIKEY\": API_KEY,\n        \"X-AUTH-SIGNATURE\": signature\n    }\n\n    response = requests.post(\n        url,\n        data=payload,\n        headers=headers\n    )\n\n    print(response.text)\n\n    return response.json()\n\n# =========================================\n# /balance\n# =========================================\nasync def balance(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n    balances = get_balances()\n\n    # API ERROR CHECK\n    if isinstance(balances, dict):\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            f\"\u274c API ERROR\\n\\n{balances}\"\n        )\n\n        return\n\n    # GET MARKET DATA\n    ticker_url = \"https://api.coindcx.com/exchange/ticker\"\n\n    market_data = requests.get(ticker_url).json()\n\n    message = \"\ud83d\udcb0 LIVE PORTFOLIO\\n\\n\"\n\n    total_value = 0\n\n    for asset in balances:\n\n        # INCLUDE LOCKED BALANCE\n        qty = (\n            float(asset['balance']) +\n            float(asset['locked_balance'])\n        )\n\n        if qty > 0:\n\n            coin = asset['currency'].upper()\n\n            live_price = 0\n            inr_value = 0\n\n            # INR WALLET\n            if coin == \"INR\":\n\n                live_price = 1\n                inr_value = qty\n\n            else:\n\n                found_market = False\n\n                # =========================\n                # TRY INR MARKET\n                # =========================\n\n                market_name_inr = coin + \"INR\"\n\n                for market in market_data:\n\n                    if market['market'] == market_name_inr:\n\n                        live_price = float(market['last_price'])\n\n                        inr_value = qty * live_price\n\n                        found_market = True\n\n                        break\n\n                # =========================\n                # TRY USDT MARKET\n                # =========================\n\n                if not found_market:\n\n                    market_name_usdt = coin + \"USDT\"\n\n                    usdt_price = 0\n                    usdt_inr = 0\n\n                    # COIN \u2192 USDT\n                    for market in market_data:\n\n                        if market['market'] == market_name_usdt:\n\n                            usdt_price = float(market['last_price'])\n\n                            break\n\n                    # USDT \u2192 INR\n                    for market in market_data:\n\n                        if market['market'] == \"USDTINR\":\n\n                            usdt_inr = float(market['last_price'])\n\n                            break\n\n                    # FINAL INR CONVERSION\n                    if usdt_price > 0 and usdt_inr > 0:\n\n                        live_price = usdt_price * usdt_inr\n\n                        inr_value = qty * live_price\n\n            total_value += inr_value\n\n            message += (\n                f\"\ud83e\ude99 {coin}\\n\"\n                f\"Qty: {round(qty, 4)}\\n\"\n                f\"Live Price: \u20b9{round(live_price, 2)}\\n\"\n                f\"Value: \u20b9{round(inr_value, 2)}\\n\\n\"\n            )\n\n    message += f\"\ud83d\udcb5 TOTAL VALUE: \u20b9{round(total_value, 2)}\"\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(message)\n\n# =========================================\n# UPDATE PRICE HISTORY\n# =========================================\n\ndef update_price_history(symbol, price):\n\n    symbol = symbol.upper()\n\n    if symbol not in price_history:\n\n        price_history[symbol] = []\n\n    price_history[symbol].append(price)\n\n    # KEEP LAST 30 PRICES\n    if len(price_history[symbol]) > 30:\n\n        price_history[symbol].pop(0)\n# =========================================\n# PLACE LIMIT BUY ORDER\n# =========================================\n\ndef place_buy_order(symbol, limit_price, amount_inr):\n\n    market = symbol.upper() + \"INR\"\n\n    # CALCULATE QUANTITY\n    quantity = amount_inr / limit_price\n\n    url = \"https://api.coindcx.com/exchange/v1/orders/create\"\n\n    # 'import uuid' was moved to the top of the file\n\n    body = {\n        \"side\": \"buy\",\n\n        \"order_type\": \"limit_order\",\n\n        \"market\": market,\n\n        \"price_per_unit\": str(limit_price),\n\n        \"total_quantity\": str(round(quantity, 4)),\n\n        \"timestamp\": int(time.time() * 1000),\n\n        \"client_order_id\": str(uuid.uuid4())\n    }\n\n    payload = json.dumps(body, separators=(',', ':'))\n\n    signature = hmac.new(\n        SECRET_KEY.encode(),\n        payload.encode(),\n        hashlib.sha256\n    ).hexdigest()\n\n    headers = {\n        \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\",\n        \"X-AUTH-APIKEY\": API_KEY,\n        \"X-AUTH-SIGNATURE\": signature\n    }\n\n    response = requests.post(\n        url,\n        data=payload,\n        headers=headers\n    )\n    print(response.text)\n    return response.json()\n\n#==============\n#9 ANALYSIS FNCTIONS\n# PHASE5 ANALYSIS COIN\n#========================\ndef analyze_coin(coin):\n\n    history = price_history.get(\n        coin,\n        []\n    )\n\n    if len(history) < 3:\n\n        return {\n\n            \"price\": history[-1] if history else 0,\n            \"score\":20,\n            \"signal\":\"COLLECTING DATA\",\n            \"trend\":0,\n            \"support1\":0,\n            \"support2\":0,\n            \"move\":0\n        }\n\n    current=history[-1]\n\n    short_avg=sum(\n        history[-min(5,len(history)):]\n    )/min(5,len(history))\n\n    long_avg=sum(\n        history\n    )/len(history)\n\n    first=history[0]\n\n    move=((current-first)/first)*100 if first>0 else 0\n\n    support1=current*0.95\n    support2=current*0.90\n\n    score=50\n\n    if short_avg>long_avg:\n        score+=20\n\n    if move>3:\n        score+=15\n\n    if move>7:\n        score+=10\n\n    score = min(score, 100)\n\n    # \u2500\u2500 Market Intelligence modifier (PASS 10) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n    # Adjusts individual coin score based on broader market regime.\n    # market_context is populated by market_intelligence() \u2014 safe to\n    # read here as it defaults to modifier=0 until first populated.\n    market_mod = market_context.get(\"confidence_modifier\", 0)\n    score = max(0, min(100, score + market_mod))\n    # \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n\n    if score >= 85:\n        signal = \"\ud83d\ude80 STRONG BUY\"\n\n    elif score >= 70:\n        signal = \"\ud83d\udfe2 BUY\"\n\n    elif score >= 60:\n        signal = \"\ud83d\udfe1 WATCH\"\n\n    else:\n        signal = \"\ud83d\udd34 SKIP\"\n\n    return {\n\n        \"price\":round(current,2),\n        \"score\":score,\n        \"signal\":signal,\n        \"trend\":round(short_avg,2),\n        \"support1\":round(support1,2),\n        \"support2\":round(support2,2),\n        \"move\":round(move,2)\n\n    }\n\n# =========================================\n# SCORE COMMAND\n# =========================================\n\nasync def score(\n    update: Update,\n    context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE\n):\n\n\n    if len(context.args)==0:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n\n            \"Usage:\\n/score sol\"\n\n        )\n\n        return\n\n\n    coin = context.args[0].upper()\n\n\n    result = analyze_coin(\n\n        coin\n\n    )\n\n\n    message=(\n\n        f\"\ud83d\udcca SCORE REPORT\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"Coin: {coin}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Price: \u20b9{result['price']}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Score: {result['score']}/100\\n\"\n\n        f\"Signal: {result['signal']}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Trend: \u20b9{round(result['trend'],2)}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Move: {round(result['move'],2)}%\\n\\n\"\n\n\n        f\"Support1: \u20b9{round(result['support1'],2)}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Support2: \u20b9{round(result['support2'],2)}\"\n\n    )\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n\n        message\n\n    )\n\n\n\n\n# =========================================\n# /ema\n# =========================================\n\nasync def ema(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n    if len(context.args) == 0:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"Usage:\\n/ema sol\"\n        )\n\n        return\n\n    coin = context.args[0].upper()\n\n    result = analyze_live_ema(coin)\n\n    message = (\n        f\"\ud83d\udcc8 LIVE EMA ANALYSIS\\n\\n\"\n        f\"{coin}\\n\\n\"\n        f\"{result}\"\n    )\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(message)\n\n# =========================================\n# SIMPLE EMA\n# =========================================\n\ndef calculate_simple_ema(prices):\n\n    if len(prices) == 0:\n\n        return 0\n\n    return sum(prices) / len(prices)\n  # =========================================\n# LIVE EMA ANALYSIS\n# =========================================\n\ndef analyze_live_ema(symbol):\n\n    symbol = symbol.upper()\n\n    if symbol not in price_history:\n\n        return \"\u274c No data yet\"\n\n    prices = price_history[symbol]\n\n    if len(prices) < 10:\n\n        return \"\u23f3 Collecting market data...\"\n\n    short_ema = calculate_simple_ema(\n        prices[-5:]\n    )\n\n    long_ema = calculate_simple_ema(\n        prices[-10:]\n    )\n\n    # TREND\n    if short_ema > long_ema:\n\n        trend = \"\ud83d\udfe2 Bullish\"\n\n    else:\n\n        trend = \"\ud83d\udd34 Bearish\"\n\n    return (\n        f\"{trend}\\n\\n\"\n        f\"EMA5: \u20b9{round(short_ema,2)}\\n\"\n        f\"EMA10: \u20b9{round(long_ema,2)}\"\n    )\n\n\n # =========================================\n# MOMENTUM ENGINE\n# =========================================\n\nasync def momentum(update: Update,\n                   context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n    if len(context.args) == 0:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"Usage:\\n/momentum sol\"\n        )\n\n        return\n\n\n    coin = context.args[0].upper()\n\n    history = price_history.get(\n        coin,\n        []\n    )\n\n\n    if len(history) < 10:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            f\"\u23f3 Collecting data...\\n\"\n            f\"{len(history)}/10\"\n        )\n\n        return\n\n\n    current = history[-1]\n\n    old = history[-10]\n\n    move = (\n        ((current-old)/old)\n        *100\n    )\n\n\n    message = (\n\n        f\"\u26a1 {coin} MOMENTUM\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"10-period Move:\\n\"\n        f\"{round(move,2)}%\\n\\n\"\n    )\n\n\n    if move > 5:\n\n        message += (\n            \"\ud83d\ude80 Strong bullish momentum\"\n        )\n\n    elif move > 2:\n\n        message += (\n            \"\ud83d\udfe2 Positive momentum\"\n        )\n\n    elif move > -2:\n\n        message += (\n            \"\ud83d\udfe1 Sideways movement\"\n        )\n\n    else:\n\n        message += (\n            \"\ud83d\udd34 Negative momentum\"\n        )\n\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n        message\n    )\n#=========================================\n# MULTI TIMEFRAME ANALYSIS\n# =========================================\n\nasync def trend(update: Update,\n                context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n    if len(context.args) == 0:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"Usage:\\n/trend sol\"\n        )\n\n        return\n\n\n    coin = context.args[0].upper()\n\n\n    history = price_history.get(\n        coin,\n        []\n    )\n\n\n    if len(history) < 20:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"\u23f3 Need more market data\"\n        )\n\n        return\n\n\n    short_avg = sum(\n        history[-5:]\n    ) / 5\n\n    medium_avg = sum(\n        history[-10:]\n    ) / 10\n\n    long_avg = sum(\n        history[-20:]\n    ) / 20\n\n\n    message = (\n        f\"\ud83d\udcca {coin} TREND REPORT\\n\\n\"\n    )\n\n\n    message += (\n        f\"Short Avg: \u20b9{round(short_avg,2)}\\n\"\n        f\"Medium Avg: \u20b9{round(medium_avg,2)}\\n\"\n        f\"Long Avg: \u20b9{round(long_avg,2)}\\n\\n\"\n    )\n\n\n    score = 0\n\n\n    if short_avg > medium_avg:\n\n        score += 1\n        message += \"\ud83d\udfe2 Short trend bullish\\n\"\n\n    else:\n\n        message += \"\ud83d\udd34 Short trend bearish\\n\"\n\n\n    if medium_avg > long_avg:\n\n        score += 1\n        message += \"\ud83d\udfe2 Medium trend bullish\\n\"\n\n    else:\n\n        message += \"\ud83d\udd34 Medium trend bearish\\n\"\n\n\n    if score == 2:\n\n        message += (\n            \"\\n\ud83d\ude80 STRONG UPTREND\"\n        )\n\n    elif score == 1:\n\n        message += (\n            \"\\n\ud83d\udfe1 MIXED TREND\"\n        )\n\n    else:\n\n        message += (\n            \"\\n\ud83d\udd34 DOWNTREND\"\n        )\n\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n        message\n    )\n    # =========================================\n# SUPPORT / RESISTANCE\n# =========================================\n\nasync def levels(update: Update,\n                 context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n    if len(context.args) == 0:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"Usage:\\n/levels sol\"\n        )\n\n        return\n\n\n    coin = context.args[0].upper()\n\n    history = price_history.get(\n        coin,\n        []\n    )\n\n\n    if len(history) < 10:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            f\"\u23f3 Collecting data...\\n\"\n            f\"{len(history)}/10\"\n        )\n\n        return\n\n\n    support = min(\n        history[-10:]\n    )\n\n    resistance = max(\n        history[-10:]\n    )\n\n    current = history[-1]\n\n    message = (\n\n        f\"\ud83d\udcca {coin} LEVELS\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"Current: \u20b9{round(current,2)}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Support: \u20b9{round(support,2)}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Resistance: \u20b9{round(resistance,2)}\\n\\n\"\n    )\n\n\n    distance_support = (\n        ((current-support)/support)\n        *100\n    )\n\n    distance_resistance = (\n        ((resistance-current)/current)\n        *100\n    )\n\n\n    if distance_support < 3:\n\n        message += (\n            \"\ud83d\udfe2 Near support zone\"\n        )\n\n    elif distance_resistance < 3:\n\n        message += (\n            \"\ud83d\udd34 Near resistance\"\n        )\n\n    else:\n\n        message += (\n            \"\ud83d\udfe1 Between zones\"\n        )\n\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n        message\n    )\n    #==========================================\n# volatility alert engine\n#==========================================\nprice_history = {}\n# =========================================\n# VOLATILITY\n# =========================================\n\nasync def volatility(\n    update: Update,\n    context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE\n):\n\n\n    if len(context.args)==0:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n\n            \"Usage:\\n/volatility sol\"\n\n        )\n\n        return\n\n\n    coin = context.args[0].upper()\n\n\n    history = price_history.get(\n\n        coin,\n        []\n\n    )\n\n\n    if len(history) < 5:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n\n            \"\u26a0 Not enough price data\"\n\n        )\n\n        return\n\n\n    high = max(\n\n        history[-20:]\n\n    )\n\n\n    low = min(\n\n        history[-20:]\n\n    )\n\n\n    current = history[-1]\n\n\n    volatility_percent = round(\n\n        (\n\n            (\n\n                high\n                -\n                low\n\n            )\n\n            /\n\n            current\n\n        )\n\n        *100,\n\n        2\n\n    )\n\n\n    message=(\n\n        \"\ud83d\udcc8 VOLATILITY REPORT\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"Coin: {coin}\\n\"\n\n        f\"High: \u20b9{round(high,2)}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Low: \u20b9{round(low,2)}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Current: \u20b9{round(current,2)}\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"Volatility: {volatility_percent}%\"\n\n    )\n\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n\n        message\n\n    )\n\n\n#=======================================\n#smart support zone function\n#=======================================\nasync def support(update, context):\n\n    coin = context.args[0].upper()\n\n    ticker_url = \"https://api.coindcx.com/exchange/ticker\"\n    data = requests.get(ticker_url).json()\n\n    current_price = None\n\n    for market in data:\n        if market[\"market\"] == coin + \"INR\":\n            current_price = float(market[\"last_price\"])\n            break\n\n    if current_price is None:\n        await update.message.reply_text(\"Coin not found\")\n        return\n\n    support1 = round(current_price * 0.95,2)\n    support2 = round(current_price * 0.90,2)\n\n    msg = (\n        f\"\ud83d\udcca {coin} SUPPORT ZONES\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Current: \u20b9{current_price}\\n\"\n        f\"Zone 1: \u20b9{support1}\\n\"\n        f\"Zone 2: \u20b9{support2}\\n\"\n        f\"Potential dip-buy area\"\n    )\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(msg)\n    # =========================================\n# SIGNAL ENGINE\n# =========================================\n\nasync def signal(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n    if len(context.args) == 0:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"Usage:\\n/signal sol\"\n        )\n\n        return\n\n    coin = context.args[0].upper()\n\n    current_price = get_cached_price_safe(coin)\n\n    if current_price <= 0:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"Coin not found\"\n        )\n\n        return\n\n    signal_text = (\n        f\"\ud83d\udea8 {coin} SIGNAL REPORT\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Price: \u20b9{round(current_price,2)}\\n\\n\"\n    )\n\n    # EMA\n    ema_result = analyze_live_ema(coin)\n\n    signal_text += f\"{ema_result}\\n\\n\"\n\n    # Support zones\n    support1 = current_price * 0.95\n    support2 = current_price * 0.90\n\n    signal_text += (\n        f\"Support 1: \u20b9{round(support1,2)}\\n\"\n        f\"Support 2: \u20b9{round(support2,2)}\\n\\n\"\n    )\n\n    # Decision logic\n    if \"No data\" in ema_result:\n\n       signal_text += (\n        \"\u23f3 Collecting market data...\"\n    )\n\n    elif \"Bullish\" in ema_result:\n\n      signal_text += (\n        \"\ud83d\udfe2 Possible accumulation zone\"\n    )\n\n    else:\n\n      signal_text += (\n        \"\ud83d\udd34 Trend weak \u2014 monitor closely\"\n    )\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n        signal_text\n    )\n# =========================================\n# STRATEGY MODES\n# =========================================\n\ncurrent_mode = \"BALANCED\"\n\nMODES = {\n\n    \"CONSERVATIVE\": {\n        \"risk\":0.20,\n        \"grid_levels\":3,\n        \"support\":0.95\n    },\n\n    \"BALANCED\": {\n        \"risk\":0.40,\n        \"grid_levels\":5,\n        \"support\":0.93\n    },\n\n    \"AGGRESSIVE\": {\n        \"risk\":0.60,\n        \"grid_levels\":7,\n        \"support\":0.90\n    },\n\n    \"PHASE5\": {\n        \"risk\":0.50,\n        \"grid_levels\":5,\n        \"support\":0.92\n    }\n\n}\n# =========================================\n# CHANGE MODE\n# =========================================\n\nasync def mode(update: Update,\n               context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n    global current_mode\n\n    if len(context.args)==0:\n\n        msg = (\n            f\"Current Mode: {current_mode}\\n\\n\"\n            f\"Available:\\n\"\n            f\"CONSERVATIVE\\n\"\n            f\"BALANCED\\n\"\n            f\"AGGRESSIVE\\n\"\n            f\"PHASE5\"\n        )\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(msg)\n\n        return\n\n    new_mode = context.args[0].upper()\n\n    if new_mode not in MODES:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"Invalid mode\"\n        )\n\n        return\n\n    current_mode = new_mode\n\n    settings = MODES[current_mode]\n\n    msg = (\n\n        f\"\u2705 Mode Changed\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Mode: {current_mode}\\n\"\n        f\"Risk: {settings['risk']}\\n\"\n        f\"Grid: {settings['grid_levels']}\"\n    )\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(msg)\n\n\n# =========================================\n# 10 MARKET MODE\n#=======================\n#10.1 marketmode\n# =========================================\n\ndef market_mode():\n\n    btc = analyze_coin(\n        \"BTC\"\n    )\n\n\n    score = btc.get(\n        \"score\",\n        0\n    )\n\n\n    # =========================\n    # MARKET CONDITIONS\n    # =========================\n\n    if score >= 80:\n\n        return {\n\n            \"mode\":\"\ud83d\udfe2 BULL\",\n\n            \"multiplier\":1.0,\n\n            \"risk\":\"HIGH\"\n\n        }\n\n\n    elif score >= 60:\n\n        return {\n\n            \"mode\":\"\ud83d\udfe1 SIDEWAYS\",\n\n            \"multiplier\":0.60,\n\n            \"risk\":\"MEDIUM\"\n\n        }\n\n\n    else:\n\n        return {\n\n            \"mode\":\"\ud83d\udd34 BEAR\",\n\n            \"multiplier\":0.30,\n\n            \"risk\":\"SAFE\"\n\n        }\n\n#\n#==========================\n#11 risk function\n#===========================\n  # =========================================\n# 11.1 RISK CHECK\n# =========================================\n\ndef risk_check(score):\n\n    global positions\n    global virtual_balance\n\n\n    max_positions = PHASE5[\n        \"trade\"\n    ][\n        \"max_positions\"\n    ]\n\n\n    # Maximum positions limit\n\n    if len(\n        positions\n    ) >= max_positions:\n\n        return (\n\n            False,\n\n            \"\u274c Maximum positions reached\"\n\n        )\n\n\n    # Balance check\n\n    if virtual_balance < 500:\n\n        return (\n\n            False,\n\n            \"\u274c Balance too low\"\n\n        )\n\n\n    # Signal quality check\n\n    if score < 60:\n\n        return (\n\n            False,\n\n            \"\u274c Score too weak\"\n\n        )\n\n\n    return (\n\n        True,\n\n        \"\u2705 Risk passed\"\n\n    )\n# =========================================\n# RISK METER\n# =========================================\n\nasync def risk(\n    update: Update,\n    context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE\n):\n\n\n    if len(context.args)==0:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n\n            \"Usage:\\n/risk sol\"\n\n        )\n\n        return\n\n\n    coin = context.args[0].upper()\n\n\n    result = analyze_coin(\n\n        coin\n\n    )\n\n\n    score = result[\n        \"score\"\n    ]\n\n\n    risk_level = \"\"\n\n\n    if score >= 80:\n\n        risk_level = \"\ud83d\udfe2 LOW RISK\"\n\n\n    elif score >= 60:\n\n        risk_level = \"\ud83d\udfe1 MEDIUM RISK\"\n\n\n    else:\n\n        risk_level = \"\ud83d\udd34 HIGH RISK\"\n\n\n    allowed,reason = risk_check(\n\n        score\n\n    )\n\n\n    message=(\n\n        \"\u26a0 RISK REPORT\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"Coin: {coin}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Score: {score}/100\\n\"\n\n        f\"Risk: {risk_level}\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"{reason}\"\n\n    )\n\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n\n        message\n\n    )\n\n# =========================================\n# RISK ENGINE\n# =========================================\n\nasync def risk_engine(update: Update,\n               context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n    if len(context.args) < 2:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"Usage:\\n/positionplan sol 1000\"\n        )\n\n        return\n\n    coin = context.args[0].upper()\n\n    capital = float(\n        context.args[1]\n    )\n\n    current_price = get_cached_price_safe(coin)\n\n    if current_price <= 0:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"Coin not found\"\n        )\n\n        return\n\n    # Phase 5 allocation model\n    safe = capital * 0.20\n    moderate = capital * 0.40\n    aggressive = capital * 0.60\n\n    message = (\n\n        f\"\ud83d\udcb0 {coin} POSITION PLAN\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"Capital: \u20b9{capital}\\n\"\n        f\"Price: \u20b9{round(current_price,2)}\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"\ud83d\udfe2 Safe: \u20b9{round(safe,2)}\\n\"\n        f\"\ud83d\udfe1 Moderate: \u20b9{round(moderate,2)}\\n\"\n        f\"\ud83d\udd34 Aggressive: \u20b9{round(aggressive,2)}\"\n    )\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n        message\n    )\n\n #=========================================\n# PHASE 5 EXIT PROTECTION\n# =========================================\ndef exit_check(coin):\n\n    history = price_history.get(\n        coin,\n        []\n    )\n\n\n    # Need enough history\n\n    if len(history) < 5:\n\n        return {\n\n            \"exit\":False,\n            \"reason\":\"No data\"\n\n        }\n\n\n    current = history[-1]\n\n\n    buy_price = positions.get(\n        coin,\n        {}\n    ).get(\n        \"buy_price\",\n        0\n    )\n\n\n    # Protect against instant auto sell\n\n    if buy_price > 0:\n\n        if current <= buy_price:\n\n            return {\n\n                \"exit\":False,\n                \"reason\":\"Below buy protection\"\n\n            }\n    buy_time = positions.get(\n        coin,\n          {}\n    ).get(\n        \"time\",\n         0\n    )\n\n    hold_seconds = (\n\n    time.time()\n    -\n    buy_time\n\n)\n\n    if hold_seconds < 300:\n\n            return {\n\n              \"exit\":False,\n              \"reason\":\"Hold protection active\"\n\n           }\n\n    # 3 candle average\n\n    avg3 = (\n\n        sum(\n            history[-3:]\n        )\n\n        / 3\n\n    )\n\n\n    # 5 candle average\n\n    avg5 = (\n\n        sum(\n            history[-5:]\n        )\n\n        / 5\n\n    )\n\n\n    drop = (\n\n        (\n\n            current\n            -\n            avg5\n\n        )\n\n        /\n\n        avg5\n\n    ) * 100\n\n\n    # Exit rules\n\n    if current < avg3:\n\n        return {\n\n            \"exit\":True,\n            \"reason\":\"Below short trend\"\n\n        }\n\n\n    if drop < -3:\n\n        return {\n\n            \"exit\":True,\n            \"reason\":\"3% fast drop\"\n\n        }\n\n\n    return {\n\n        \"exit\":False,\n        \"reason\":\"Healthy\"\n\n    }\n\n#11.3 CHECK COOLDOWN\n# =========================================\n\ndef check_cooldown():\n\n    global cooldown_until\n\n\n    if cooldown_until is None:\n\n        return (\n\n            False,\n\n            \"Trading active\"\n\n        )\n\n\n    now = datetime.now()\n\n\n    if now >= cooldown_until:\n\n        cooldown_until = None\n\n        return (\n\n            False,\n\n            \"Trading active\"\n\n        )\n\n\n    remaining = (\n\n        cooldown_until\n        -\n        now\n\n    )\n\n\n    minutes = max(\n\n        0,\n\n        int(\n            remaining.total_seconds()\n            / 60\n        )\n\n    )\n\n\n    return (\n\n        True,\n\n        f\"Cooldown active ({minutes} min left)\"\n    )\n\n#================================\n#11.4 update_loss_streak\n#================================\ndef update_loss_streak(pnl):\n\n    global loss_streak\n    global cooldown_until\n\n\n    if pnl < 0:\n\n        loss_streak += 1\n\n    else:\n\n        loss_streak = 0\n\n\n    if loss_streak >= 3:\n\n        cooldown_until = (\n\n            datetime.now()\n\n            +\n\n            timedelta(\n                hours=2\n            )\n\n        )\n\n\n        loss_streak = 0\n\n#======================================\n #10.2 CAPITAL ALLOCATION\n# =========================================\n\ndef legacy_market_get_allocation(score):\n\n\n    global virtual_balance\n\n\n    market = market_mode()\n\n\n    multiplier = market[\n        \"multiplier\"\n    ]\n\n\n    if score >= 85:\n\n        base = (\n\n            virtual_balance\n            *\n            0.40\n\n        )\n\n\n    elif score >= 70:\n\n        base = (\n\n            virtual_balance\n            *\n            0.25\n\n        )\n\n\n    elif score >= 60:\n\n        base = (\n\n            virtual_balance\n            *\n            0.15\n\n        )\n\n\n    else:\n\n        base = (\n\n            virtual_balance\n            *\n            0.05\n\n        )\n\n\n    amount = (\n\n        base\n        *\n        multiplier\n\n    )\n\n\n    return {\n\n        \"amount\":round(\n            amount,\n            2\n        ),\n\n        \"mode\":market[\n            \"risk\"\n        ],\n\n        \"market\":market[\n            \"mode\"\n        ]\n\n  }\n  # =========================================\n# 12.1 CAPITAL ALLOCATION ENGINE\n# =========================================\n\ndef get_allocation(score):\n\n    global virtual_balance\n\n    risk = PHASE5[\"risk\"]\n\n    if score >= 85:\n\n        allocation = risk[\n            \"aggressive\"\n        ]\n\n        mode = \"AGGRESSIVE\"\n\n    elif score >= 65:\n\n        allocation = risk[\n            \"moderate\"\n        ]\n\n        mode = \"MODERATE\"\n\n    else:\n\n        allocation = risk[\n            \"safe\"\n        ]\n\n        mode = \"SAFE\"\n\n\n    amount = (\n\n        virtual_balance\n\n        *\n\n        allocation\n\n    )\n\n\n    return {\n\n        \"amount\":round(amount,2),\n\n        \"mode\":mode\n\n    }\n\n# =========================================\n# 13.1TRADE LOG SYSTEM\n# =========================================\n\n# datetime already imported at top of file as: from datetime import datetime\ntrade_log = []\n\n\ndef log_trade(\n    coin,\n    action,\n    price,\n    amount,\n    pnl=0\n):\n\n    global trade_log\n\n\n    trade_log.append({\n\n        \"time\":\n        datetime.now()\n        .strftime(\n            \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\"\n        ),\n\n        \"coin\":\n        coin,\n\n        \"action\":\n        action,\n\n        \"price\":\n        round(\n            price,\n            2\n        ),\n\n        \"amount\":\n        round(\n            amount,\n            2\n        ),\n\n        \"pnl\":\n        round(\n            pnl,\n            2\n        )\n\n    })\n # =========================================\n# UPDATE STATS\n# =========================================\ndef update_stats(\n    trade_type,\n    pnl\n):\n\n    global trade_stats\n\n\n    if trade_type == \"SELL\":\n\n        # Total trades\n\n        trade_stats[\n            \"total_trades\"\n        ] += 1\n\n\n        # Wins / losses\n\n        if pnl > 0:\n\n            trade_stats[\n                \"wins\"\n            ] += 1\n\n\n            # Win streak\n\n            trade_stats[\n                \"win_streak\"\n            ] += 1\n\n\n            trade_stats[\n                \"loss_streak\"\n            ] = 0\n\n\n        else:\n\n            trade_stats[\n                \"losses\"\n            ] += 1\n\n\n            # Loss streak\n\n            trade_stats[\n                \"loss_streak\"\n            ] += 1\n\n\n            trade_stats[\n                \"win_streak\"\n            ] = 0\n\n\n        # Total PnL\n\n        trade_stats[\n            \"total_profit\"\n        ] += pnl\n\n\n        # Best trade\n\n        if pnl > trade_stats[\n            \"best_trade\"\n        ]:\n\n            trade_stats[\n                \"best_trade\"\n            ] = pnl\n\n\n        # Worst trade\n\n        if pnl < trade_stats[\n            \"worst_trade\"\n        ]:\n\n            trade_stats[\n                \"worst_trade\"\n            ] = pnl\n\n\n    save_data()\n\n# =========================================\n# UPDATE PERFORMANCE\n# =========================================\n\ndef update_performance(\n    pnl\n):\n\n    global performance_data\n\n\n    # Total profit/loss\n\n    if pnl > 0:\n\n        performance_data[\n            \"total_profit\"\n        ] += pnl\n\n    else:\n\n        performance_data[\n            \"total_loss\"\n        ] += abs(pnl)\n\n\n    # Best trade\n\n    if pnl > performance_data[\n        \"best_trade\"\n    ]:\n\n        performance_data[\n            \"best_trade\"\n        ] = pnl\n\n\n    # Worst trade\n\n    if pnl < performance_data[\n        \"worst_trade\"\n    ]:\n\n        performance_data[\n            \"worst_trade\"\n        ] = pnl\n\n\n    # Win streak\n\n    if pnl > 0:\n\n        performance_data[\n            \"win_streak\"\n        ] += 1\n\n        performance_data[\n            \"loss_streak\"\n        ] = 0\n\n\n    else:\n\n        performance_data[\n            \"loss_streak\"\n        ] += 1\n\n        performance_data[\n            \"win_streak\"\n        ] = 0\n\n\n    save_data()\n# =========================================\n# RESET PAPER ACCOUNT\n# =========================================\n\nasync def reset(update: Update,\n                context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n\n    global positions\n    global virtual_balance\n    global trade_log\n\n\n    positions = {}\n\n    trade_log = []\n\n    virtual_balance = 10000\n\n\n    save_data()\n\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n\n        \"\ud83d\udd04 PHASE 5 RESET\\n\\n\"\n\n        \"Balance: \u20b910000\\n\"\n\n        \"Positions cleared\\n\"\n\n        \"Trade history cleared\"\n\n    )\n\n# =========================================\n# PERFORMANCE REPORT\n# =========================================\n\nasync def performance_report(\n    update: Update,\n    context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n    global trade_stats\n\n\n    total = trade_stats[\n        \"total_trades\"\n    ]\n\n\n    wins = trade_stats[\n        \"wins\"\n    ]\n\n\n    losses = trade_stats[\n        \"losses\"\n    ]\n\n\n    profit = trade_stats[\n        \"total_profit\"\n    ]\n\n\n    if total == 0:\n\n        winrate = 0\n\n    else:\n\n        winrate = round(\n\n            (\n                wins\n                /\n                total\n            )\n            *\n            100,\n\n            2\n\n        )\n\n\n    message=(\n\n        \"\ud83d\udcca PERFORMANCE REPORT\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"Trades: {total}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Wins: {wins}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Losses: {losses}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Win Rate: {winrate}%\\n\"\n\n        f\"Net PnL: \u20b9{round(profit,2)}\"\n\n    )\n\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n        message\n    )\n\n\n #======================================\n#14 BUY fnctions\n#=======================================\n# 14.1 paper buy function\n#=======================================\n# =========================================\n# PAPER BUY\n# =========================================\n\nasync def buy(update: Update,\n              context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n    global virtual_balance\n    global positions\n\n\n    if len(context.args) < 2:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"Usage:\\n/buy sol 500\"\n        )\n\n        return\n\n\n    coin = context.args[0].upper()\n\n    amount = float(\n        context.args[1]\n    )\n\n\n    price = get_cached_price_safe(coin)\n\n\n    # =========================\n    # COOLDOWN CHECK\n    # =========================\n\n    cooldown, reason = check_cooldown()\n\n    if cooldown:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n\n            f\"\u26d4 Trade blocked\\n\\n\"\n            f\"{reason}\"\n\n        )\n\n        return\n\n\n    qty = amount / price\n\n\n    virtual_balance -= amount\n\n\n    positions[coin] = {\n\n        \"buy_price\": price,\n        \"qty\": qty,\n        \"amount\": amount,\n        \"time\":time.time(),\n        \"peak\":price,\n        \"trailing_active\":False\n\n    }\n    log_trade(\n       coin,\n       \"buy\",\n       price,\n       amount\n    )\n    update_portfolio_history()\n    save_data()\n\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n\n        f\"\u2705 PAPER BUY\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"{coin}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Price: \u20b9{round(price,2)}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Qty: {round(qty,6)}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Balance: \u20b9{round(virtual_balance,2)}\"\n\n    )\n\n   #======================================\n#15.SELL fnctions\n#=======================================\n# 15.1 paper buy function\n#=======================================\n# =========================================\n# PAPER sell\n# =========================================\n# =========================================\n# PAPER SELL\n# =========================================\n\nasync def sell(update: Update,\n               context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n\n    global virtual_balance\n    global positions\n\n\n    if len(context.args) < 2:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n\n            \"Usage:\\n/sell sol 150\"\n\n        )\n\n        return\n\n\n    coin = context.args[0].upper()\n\n    sell_amount = float(\n        context.args[1]\n    )\n\n\n    if coin not in positions:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n\n            \"\u274c No position\"\n\n        )\n\n        return\n\n\n    current = get_cached_price_safe(coin)\n\n\n    buy_price = positions[\n        coin\n    ][\n        \"buy_price\"\n    ]\n\n\n    qty = positions[\n        coin\n    ][\n        \"qty\"\n    ]\n\n\n    total_amount = positions[\n        coin\n    ][\n        \"amount\"\n    ]\n\n\n    current_value = (\n\n        qty\n        *\n        current\n\n    )\n\n\n    # Cannot sell more than position value\n\n    if sell_amount > current_value:\n\n        sell_amount = current_value\n\n\n    sell_qty = (\n\n        sell_amount\n        /\n        current\n\n    )\n\n\n    pnl = (\n\n        (\n            current\n            -\n            buy_price\n        )\n        *\n        sell_qty\n\n    )\n\n    update_loss_streak(pnl)\n    virtual_balance += sell_amount\n\n\n    remaining_qty = (\n\n        qty\n        -\n        sell_qty\n\n    )\n\n\n    remaining_amount = (\n\n        total_amount\n        -\n        sell_amount\n\n    )\n\n\n    # Position closed fully\n\n    if remaining_qty <= 0.000001:\n\n        del positions[\n            coin\n        ]\n\n    else:\n\n        positions[\n            coin\n        ] = {\n\n            \"buy_price\":\n            buy_price,\n\n            \"qty\":\n            remaining_qty,\n\n            \"amount\":\n            remaining_amount\n\n        }\n\n\n    log_trade(\n\n        coin,\n        \"SELL\",\n        current,\n        sell_amount,\n        pnl\n\n    )\n    update_stats(\n        \"SELL\",\n        pnl\n    )\n    update_portfolio_history()\n    save_data()\n\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n\n        f\"\u2705 PAPER SELL\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"{coin}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Sold: \u20b9{round(sell_amount,2)}\\n\"\n\n        f\"PnL: \u20b9{round(pnl,2)}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Remaining: \u20b9{round(remaining_amount,2)}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Balance: \u20b9{round(virtual_balance,2)}\"\n\n\n  )\n# =========================================\n# TRADE COMMAND\n# =========================================\n\nasync def trade(update: Update,\n                context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n    global trade_history\n    global trade_stats\n\n    try:\n\n        if len(context.args) < 3:\n\n            await update.message.reply_text(\n                \"Usage:\\n/trade sol buy 150\"\n            )\n            return\n\n\n        coin = context.args[0].upper()\n\n        action = context.args[1].upper()\n\n        amount = float(\n            context.args[2]\n        )\n\n\n        current_price = get_cached_price_safe(coin)\n\n\n        trade_data = {\n\n            \"coin\": coin,\n\n            \"action\": action,\n\n            \"amount\": amount,\n\n            \"price\": current_price,\n\n            \"time\": datetime.now().strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\")\n        }\n\n\n        trade_history.append(\n            trade_data\n        )\n\n\n        # ======================\n        # STATS UPDATE\n        # ======================\n\n        trade_stats[\n            \"total_trades\"\n        ] += 1\n\n\n        import random\n\n        result = random.choice(\n            [\"WIN\",\"LOSS\"]\n        )\n\n\n        if result == \"WIN\":\n\n            trade_stats[\n                \"wins\"\n            ] += 1\n\n            trade_stats[\n                \"profit\"\n            ] += amount * 0.05\n\n        else:\n\n            trade_stats[\n                \"losses\"\n            ] += 1\n\n            trade_stats[\n                \"profit\"\n            ] -= amount * 0.03\n\n\n        save_data()\n\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n\n            f\"\u2705 Trade Saved\\n\\n\"\n\n            f\"Coin: {coin}\\n\"\n\n            f\"Action: {action}\\n\"\n\n            f\"Amount: \u20b9{amount}\\n\"\n\n            f\"Price: \u20b9{round(current_price,2)}\\n\"\n\n            f\"Result: {result}\"\n\n        )\n\n    except Exception as e:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            f\"Error: {e}\"\n        )\n\n#==========================================\n  #livebuy\n# =========================================\n\nasync def livebuy(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n    # CHECK FORMAT\n    if len(context.args) < 3:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"Usage:\\n/buy sol 8500 150\"\n        )\n\n        return\n\n    coin = context.args[0].upper()\n\n    limit_price = float(context.args[1])\n\n    amount_inr = float(context.args[2])\n\n    # AUTO CALCULATE QUANTITY\n    quantity = amount_inr / limit_price\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n        f\"\u23f3 LIMIT BUY ORDER\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Coin: {coin}\\n\"\n        f\"Limit Price: \u20b9{limit_price}\\n\"\n        f\"Investment: \u20b9{amount_inr}\\n\"\n        f\"Estimated Qty: {round(quantity,4)}\"\n    )\n\n    result = place_buy_order(\n        coin,\n        limit_price,\n        amount_inr\n    )\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n        f\"\u2705 ORDER RESPONSE\\n\\n{result}\"\n    )\n\n\n#==========================\n#16 PORTFOLIO FNCTIONS\n#======================\n#16.1 PORTFOLIO\n#=========================\n# =========================================\n# PORTFOLIO\n# =========================================\n\nasync def portfolio(update: Update,\n                    context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n\n    global positions\n\n\n    if len(positions) == 0:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n\n            \"\ud83d\udcc2 No open positions\"\n\n        )\n\n        return\n\n\n    message = (\n\n        \"\ud83d\udcca PORTFOLIO\\n\\n\"\n\n    )\n\n\n    total_value = 0\n\n\n    for coin,data in positions.items():\n\n        current = get_cached_price_safe(coin)\n\n\n        qty = data[\n            \"qty\"\n        ]\n\n\n        buy_price = data[\n            \"buy_price\"\n        ]\n\n\n        value = (\n\n            current\n            *\n            qty\n\n        )\n\n\n        pnl = (\n\n            value\n            -\n            data[\n                \"amount\"\n            ]\n\n        )\n\n\n        total_value += value\n\n\n        message += (\n\n            f\"{coin}\\n\"\n\n            f\"Buy: \u20b9{round(buy_price,2)}\\n\"\n\n            f\"Current: \u20b9{round(current,2)}\\n\"\n\n            f\"Qty: {round(qty,6)}\\n\"\n\n            f\"PnL: \u20b9{round(pnl,2)}\\n\\n\"\n\n        )\n\n\n    message += (\n\n        f\"Total Value: \u20b9\"\n\n        f\"{round(total_value,2)}\"\n\n    )\n\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n        message\n    )\n    # =========================================\n# 16.2 PAPER HISTORY\n# =========================================\n\nasync def paper(update: Update,\n                context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n    message = (\n        \"\ud83d\udcd2 PAPER HISTORY\\n\\n\"\n    )\n\n\n    recent = paper_history[-10:]\n\n\n    for trade in recent:\n\n        message += (\n\n            f\"{trade['coin']} \"\n\n            f\"{trade['action']}\\n\"\n        )\n\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n        message\n    )\n\n# =========================================\n# 16.3 POSITION ANALYTICS\n# =========================================\n\nasync def position(update: Update,\n                   context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n    global positions\n\n    if len(positions) == 0:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"\ud83d\udcca No open positions\"\n        )\n\n        return\n\n\n    message = \"\ud83d\udcc8 POSITION ANALYTICS\\n\\n\"\n\n\n    for coin,data in positions.items():\n\n        buy_price = data[\n            \"buy_price\"\n        ]\n\n        qty = data[\n            \"qty\"\n        ]\n\n        amount = data[\n            \"amount\"\n        ]\n\n\n        current = get_cached_price_safe(coin)\n\n\n        value = current * qty\n\n\n        pnl = (\n            value\n            -\n            amount\n        )\n\n\n        pnl_percent = (\n            pnl\n            /\n            amount\n        ) * 100\n\n\n        if pnl_percent > 5:\n\n            status = \"\ud83d\ude80 Strong Gain\"\n\n        elif pnl_percent > 0:\n\n            status = \"\ud83d\udfe2 Profit\"\n\n        elif pnl_percent > -5:\n\n            status = \"\ud83d\udfe1 Small Loss\"\n\n        else:\n\n            status = \"\ud83d\udd34 Heavy Loss\"\n\n\n        message += (\n\n            f\"{coin}\\n\"\n\n            f\"Entry: \u20b9{round(buy_price,2)}\\n\"\n\n            f\"Current: \u20b9{round(current,2)}\\n\"\n\n            f\"Qty: {round(qty,6)}\\n\"\n\n            f\"Value: \u20b9{round(value,2)}\\n\"\n\n            f\"PnL: \u20b9{round(pnl,2)} \"\n            f\"({round(pnl_percent,2)}%)\\n\"\n\n            f\"{status}\\n\\n\"\n        )\n\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n        message\n    )\n #==================================\n#17 pnl functions\n#====================================\n# =========================================\n# 17.1 LIVE PAPER PNL\n# =========================================\n\nasync def paper_pnl(update: Update,\n              context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n    global positions\n\n    if len(positions) == 0:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"\ud83d\udcca No open positions\"\n        )\n\n        return\n\n\n    message = \"\ud83d\udcca OPEN POSITION PNL\\n\\n\"\n\n    total_pnl = 0\n\n\n    for coin,data in positions.items():\n\n        current = get_cached_price_safe(coin)\n\n        buy_price = data[\n            \"buy_price\"\n        ]\n\n        qty = data[\n            \"qty\"\n        ]\n\n\n        pnl_value = (\n\n            current\n            -\n            buy_price\n\n        ) * qty\n\n\n        pnl_percent = (\n\n            (\n                current\n                -\n                buy_price\n            )\n\n            /\n\n            buy_price\n\n        ) * 100\n\n\n        total_pnl += pnl_value\n\n\n        message += (\n\n            f\"{coin}\\n\"\n\n            f\"Buy: \u20b9{round(buy_price,2)}\\n\"\n\n            f\"Current: \u20b9{round(current,2)}\\n\"\n\n            f\"PnL: \u20b9{round(pnl_value,2)} \"\n\n            f\"({round(pnl_percent,2)}%)\\n\\n\"\n        )\n\n\n    message += (\n\n        f\"\ud83d\udcb0 Total PnL: \"\n        f\"\u20b9{round(total_pnl,2)}\"\n    )\n\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(   message)\n#======================\n# =========================================\n# 17.2 cdx PNL\n# =========================================\n\nasync def cdx_pnl(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n    balances = get_balances()\n\n    # API ERROR CHECK\n    if isinstance(balances, dict):\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            f\"\u274c API ERROR\\n\\n{balances}\"\n        )\n\n        return\n\n    ticker_url = \"https://api.coindcx.com/exchange/ticker\"\n\n    market_data = requests.get(ticker_url).json()\n\n    message = \"\ud83d\udcca FULL PORTFOLIO PNL\\n\\n\"\n\n    total_pnl = 0\n    total_value = 0\n\n    for asset in balances:\n\n        qty = (\n            float(asset['balance']) +\n            float(asset['locked_balance'])\n        )\n\n        if qty > 0:\n\n            coin = asset['currency'].upper()\n\n            # SKIP INR\n            if coin == \"INR\":\n\n                continue\n\n            live_price = 0\n\n            found_market = False\n\n            # =========================\n            # INR MARKET\n            # =========================\n\n            market_name_inr = coin + \"INR\"\n\n            for market in market_data:\n\n                if market['market'] == market_name_inr:\n\n                    live_price = float(\n                        market['last_price']\n                    )\n\n                    found_market = True\n\n                    break\n\n            # =========================\n            # USDT MARKET\n            # =========================\n\n            if not found_market:\n\n                market_name_usdt = coin + \"USDT\"\n\n                usdt_price = 0\n                usdt_inr = 0\n\n                # COIN \u2192 USDT\n                for market in market_data:\n\n                    if market['market'] == market_name_usdt:\n\n                        usdt_price = float(\n                            market['last_price']\n                        )\n\n                        break\n\n                # USDT \u2192 INR\n                for market in market_data:\n\n                    if market['market'] == \"USDTINR\":\n\n                        usdt_inr = float(\n                            market['last_price']\n                        )\n\n                        break\n\n                if usdt_price > 0 and usdt_inr > 0:\n\n                    live_price = (\n                        usdt_price * usdt_inr\n                    )\n\n            # =========================\n            # CURRENT VALUE\n            # =========================\n\n            current_value = qty * live_price\n\n            total_value += current_value\n\n            # =========================\n            # PNL CALCULATION\n            # =========================\n\n            if coin in buy_prices:\n\n                buy_price = buy_prices[coin]\n\n                invested = qty * buy_price\n\n                pnl_value = (\n                    current_value - invested\n                )\n\n                total_pnl += pnl_value\n\n                status = \"\ud83d\udfe2\"\n\n                if pnl_value < 0:\n\n                    status = \"\ud83d\udd34\"\n\n                message += (\n                    f\"{status} {coin}\\n\"\n                    f\"Qty: {round(qty,4)}\\n\"\n                    f\"Buy Avg: \u20b9{round(buy_price,2)}\\n\"\n                    f\"Live: \u20b9{round(live_price,2)}\\n\"\n                    f\"PnL: \u20b9{round(pnl_value,2)}\\n\\n\"\n                )\n\n            else:\n\n                message += (\n                    f\"\u26aa {coin}\\n\"\n                    f\"Qty: {round(qty,4)}\\n\"\n                    f\"Live: \u20b9{round(live_price,2)}\\n\"\n                    f\"No Buy Avg Set\\n\\n\"\n                )\n\n    message += (\n        f\"\ud83d\udcb0 Portfolio Value: \u20b9{round(total_value,2)}\\n\"\n        f\"\ud83d\udcc8 Total PnL: \u20b9{round(total_pnl,2)}\"\n    )\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(message)\n#==========================================\n#18 history fnctions\n#===========================================\n# =========================================\n#18.1 trade_history\n# =========================================\n\nasync def legacy_trade_history(update: Update,\n                  context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n    if len(trade_history) == 0:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"\ud83d\udcd2 No trades found\"\n        )\n\n        return\n\n\n    message = (\n        \"\ud83d\udcd2 PHASE 5 TRADE HISTORY\\n\\n\"\n    )\n\n\n    recent = trade_history[-10:]\n\n\n    for i, trade in enumerate(\n            recent,\n            start=1):\n\n        message += (\n\n            f\"#{i}\\n\"\n\n            f\"\ud83e\ude99 {trade['coin']}\\n\"\n\n            f\"Action: {trade['action']}\\n\"\n\n            f\"Amount: \u20b9{trade['amount']}\\n\"\n\n            f\"Price: \u20b9{round(trade['price'],2)}\\n\"\n\n            f\"Time: {trade['time']}\\n\\n\"\n        )\n\n\n    message += (\n        f\"Total Trades: \"\n        f\"{len(trade_history)}\"\n    )\n\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n        message\n    )\n\n #====================================\n #19. stats fnctions\n #====================================\n #19.1 stats\n# =========================================\n\nasync def stats(update: Update,\n                context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n\n    global trade_log\n\n\n    if len(trade_log)==0:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n\n            \"\ud83d\udcca No trade data\"\n\n        )\n\n        return\n\n\n    total = len(trade_log)\n\n\n    sells = [\n\n        x for x in trade_log\n\n        if x[\"action\"]==\"SELL\"\n\n    ]\n\n\n    wins = len([\n\n        x for x in sells\n\n        if x[\"pnl\"]>0\n\n    ])\n\n\n    losses = len([\n\n        x for x in sells\n\n        if x[\"pnl\"]<=0\n\n    ])\n\n\n    total_pnl = sum([\n\n        x[\"pnl\"]\n\n        for x in sells\n\n    ])\n\n\n    winrate = (\n\n        wins / len(sells) *100\n\n        if len(sells)>0\n\n        else 0\n\n    )\n\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n\n        f\"\ud83d\udcca PHASE 5 STATS\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"Trades: {total}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Wins: {wins}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Losses: {losses}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Win Rate: {round(winrate,2)}%\\n\"\n\n        f\"Total PnL: \u20b9{round(total_pnl,2)}\"\n\n    )\n #=========================================\n #20 dashboard function\n #========================================\n # =========================================\n# DASHBOARD\n# =========================================\n\nasync def dashboard(\n    update: Update,\n    context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE\n):\n\n\n    wins = trade_stats[\n        \"wins\"\n    ]\n\n    losses = trade_stats[\n        \"losses\"\n    ]\n\n    total = trade_stats[\n        \"total_trades\"\n    ]\n\n\n    winrate = 0\n\n\n    if total > 0:\n\n        winrate = round(\n\n            (\n\n                wins\n                /\n\n                total\n\n            )\n\n            *100,\n\n            2\n\n        )\n\n\n    message=(\n\n        \"\ud83d\udcca PHASE 5 DASHBOARD\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"Trades: {total}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Wins: {wins}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Losses: {losses}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Win Rate: {winrate}%\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"Balance: \u20b9{round(virtual_balance,2)}\"\n\n    )\n\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n\n        message\n\n    )\n\n\n\n\n\n# =========================================\n# SETTINGS VIEW\n# =========================================\n\nasync def settings(update: Update,\n                   context: history(update: Update,\n                  context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n\n    if len(\n        trade_log\n    ) == 0:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"\ud83d\udcd2 No trade history\"\n        )\n\n        return\n\n\n    message = (\n        \"\ud83d\udcd2 TRADE HISTORY\\n\\n\"\n    )\n\n\n    recent = trade_log[-10:]\n\n\n    for trade in recent:\n\n        message += (\n\n            f\"{trade['time']}\\n\"\n\n            f\"{trade['coin']} \"\n\n            f\"{trade['action']}\\n\"\n\n            f\"\u20b9{trade['price']}\\n\"\n\n            f\"PnL:
