=========================================\n\n\n# =========================================\n# PORTFOLIO REPORT\n# =========================================\n\nasync def report(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n    message = \"\ud83d\udcca PHASE 5 REPORT\\n\\n\"\n\n    # Portfolio Value\n    balances = get_balances()\n\n    total_value = 0\n\n    if not isinstance(balances, dict):\n\n        ticker_url = \"https://api.coindcx.com/exchange/ticker\"\n\n        market_data = requests.get(\n            ticker_url\n        ).json()\n\n        for asset in balances:\n\n            qty = (\n                float(asset['balance']) +\n                float(asset['locked_balance'])\n            )\n\n            if qty > 0:\n\n                coin = asset['currency'].upper()\n\n                if coin == \"INR\":\n\n                    total_value += qty\n\n                else:\n\n                    market_name = coin + \"INR\"\n\n                    for market in market_data:\n\n                        if market['market'] == market_name:\n\n                            price = float(\n                                market['last_price']\n                            )\n\n                            total_value += (\n                                qty * price\n                            )\n\n                            break\n\n    message += (\n        f\"\ud83d\udcb0 Portfolio: \u20b9{round(total_value,2)}\\n\\n\"\n    )\n\n    # Watchlist trend status\n    message += \"\ud83d\udcc8 Trends\\n\\n\"\n\n    for coin in watchlist:\n\n        trend = analyze_live_ema(\n            coin\n        )\n\n        if \"Bullish\" in trend:\n\n            message += (\n                f\"\ud83d\udfe2 {coin}\\n\"\n            )\n\n        elif \"Bearish\" in trend:\n\n            message += (\n                f\"\ud83d\udd34 {coin}\\n\"\n            )\n\n        else:\n\n            message += (\n                f\"\u26aa {coin}\\n\"\n            )\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n        message\n    )\n\n    # =========================================\n# PAPER HISTORY\n# =========================================\n\nasync def legacy_paper_history(update: Update,\n                context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n\n        f\"\ud83d\udcd2 PAPER ACCOUNT\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"Balance: \u20b9{round(virtual_balance,2)}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Open Positions: \"\n\n        f\"{len(positions)}\"\n\n    )\n# =========================================\n# 20.3 portfolio snapshot\n# =========================================\n\nasync def snapshot(update: Update,\n                   context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n    balances = get_balances()\n\n    total_value = 0\n\n    if not isinstance(\n        balances,\n        dict\n    ):\n\n        market_data = requests.get(\n            \"https://api.coindcx.com/exchange/ticker\"\n        ).json()\n\n        for asset in balances:\n\n            qty = (\n                float(asset[\"balance\"])\n                +\n                float(asset[\"locked_balance\"])\n            )\n\n            if qty <= 0:\n                continue\n\n            coin = (\n                asset[\"currency\"]\n                .upper()\n            )\n\n            if coin == \"INR\":\n\n                total_value += qty\n\n            else:\n\n                market_name = (\n                    coin + \"INR\"\n                )\n\n                for market in market_data:\n\n                    if market[\"market\"] == market_name:\n\n                        price = float(\n                            market[\"last_price\"]\n                        )\n\n                        total_value += (\n                            qty * price\n                        )\n\n                        break\n\n\n    record = {\n\n        \"time\": time.strftime(\n            \"%d-%m-%Y %H:%M\"\n        ),\n\n        \"portfolio\": round(\n            total_value,\n            2\n        ),\n\n        \"trades\": len(\n            trade_history\n        )\n    }\n\n    portfolio_history.append(\n        record\n    )\n\n    save_data()\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n\n        f\"\ud83d\udcf8 Snapshot Saved\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"Portfolio: \u20b9{round(total_value,2)}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Trades: {len(trade_history)}\"\n    )\n# =========================================\n# 21 watchlist function\n# =========================================\n#21.1 show watchlist\n#==========================================\n\nasync def show_watchlist(update: Update,\n                         context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n\n    message = \"\ud83d\udccb CURRENT WATCHLIST\\n\\n\"\n\n\n    for coin in watchlist:\n\n        price = get_cached_price_safe(coin)\n\n\n        result = analyze_coin(\n            coin\n        )\n\n\n        signal = result.get(\n            \"signal\",\n            \"NO DATA\"\n        )\n\n\n        message += (\n\n            f\"\u2022 {coin}\\n\"\n\n            f\"\u20b9{round(price,2)}\\n\"\n\n            f\"{signal}\\n\\n\"\n\n        )\n\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n        message\n    )\n    # =========================================\n# /add  watchlist\n# =========================================\n\nasync def add_coin(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n    if len(context.args) == 0:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"Usage:\\n/add sol\"\n        )\n\n        return\n\n    coin = context.args[0].upper()\n\n    if coin not in watchlist:\n\n        watchlist.append(coin)\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            f\"\u2705 Added {coin}\"\n        )\n\n    else:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            f\"{coin} already exists\"\n        )\n# =========================================\n# 21.3 remove  watchlist\n# =========================================\n\nasync def remove_coin(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n    if len(context.args) == 0:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"Usage:\\n/remove sol\"\n        )\n\n        return\n\n    coin = context.args[0].upper()\n\n    if coin in watchlist:\n\n        watchlist.remove(coin)\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            f\"\u274c Removed {coin}\"\n        )\n\n    else:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            f\"{coin} not found\"\n        )\n #=========================================\n #23 scan fnctions\n #=======================================\n #23.1 smart scan\n #=======================================\n # =========================================\n\nasync def legacy_smart_scan(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n    watchlist = PHASE5[\"coins\"]\n\n    message = \"\ud83d\udea8 PHASE 5 SMART SCANNER \ud83d\udea8\\n\\n\"\n\n    for coin in watchlist:\n\n        current_price = get_cached_price_safe(coin)\n\n        if current_price > 0:\n\n            # STORE PRICE HISTORY\n            update_price_history(\n                coin,\n                current_price\n            )\n\n            message += (\n                f\"\ud83e\ude99 {coin}\\n\"\n                f\"Price: \u20b9{round(current_price,2)}\\n\"\n            )\n\n            # BASIC SIGNALS\n            if coin == \"SOL\" and current_price < 9000:\n\n                message += \"\ud83d\udfe2 SOL BUY ZONE\\n\"\n\n            if coin == \"BTC\" and current_price < 9000000:\n\n                message += \"\u26a0 BTC SUPPORT AREA\\n\"\n\n            if coin == \"ETH\" and current_price < 250000:\n\n                message += \"\ud83d\udd25 ETH DISCOUNT ZONE\\n\"\n\n            # EMA ANALYSIS\n            ema_result = analyze_live_ema(coin)\n\n            message += (\n                f\"{ema_result}\\n\"\n            )\n\n            # VOLATILITY CHECK\n            if current_price > 0:\n\n                message += \"\ud83d\udcca Active Market\\n\"\n\n            message += \"\\n\"\n\n        else:\n\n            message += (\n                f\"\u274c {coin} price unavailable\\n\\n\"\n            )\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(message)\n\n# =========================================\n# 23.2 master scan\n# =========================================\n\nasync def scan(update: Update,\n               context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n\n    if len(context.args) == 0:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"Usage:\\n/scan sol\"\n        )\n\n        return\n\n\n    coin = context.args[0].upper()\n\n\n    result = analyze_coin(\n        coin\n    )\n\n\n    current = result.get(\n        \"price\",\n        0\n    )\n\n    score = result.get(\n        \"score\",\n        0\n    )\n\n    signal = result.get(\n        \"signal\",\n        \"UNKNOWN\"\n    )\n\n    trend = result.get(\n        \"trend\",\n        0\n    )\n\n    support1 = result.get(\n        \"support1\",\n        0\n    )\n\n    support2 = result.get(\n        \"support2\",\n        0\n    )\n\n    move = result.get(\n        \"move\",\n        0\n    )\n\n\n    risk = exit_check(\n        coin\n    )\n\n\n    if risk[\"exit\"]:\n\n        signal = \"\u26a0\ufe0f EXIT\"\n\n\n    bridge_text = \"Not triggered\"\n    if (\n        PHASE5.get(\"bot\", {}).get(\"auto_trade\", False)\n        and \"BUY\" in str(signal).upper()\n    ):\n        bridge_result = receive_signal({\n            \"coin\": coin,\n            \"action\": \"BUY\",\n            \"score\": score,\n            \"source\": \"SCAN\",\n            \"timestamp\": time.time(),\n            \"raw\": result\n        })\n        if bridge_result:\n            bridge_text = (\n                f\"{bridge_result.get('result', 'UNKNOWN')} - \"\n                f\"{bridge_result.get('reason', 'No reason')}\"\n            )\n\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n\n        f\"\ud83d\udcca PHASE 5 MASTER SCAN\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"Coin: {coin}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Price: \u20b9{current}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Score: {score}/100\\n\"\n\n        f\"Signal: {signal}\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"Trend Avg: {trend}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Move: {move}%\\n\"\n\n        f\"Support1: \u20b9{support1}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Support2: \u20b9{support2}\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"Exit Status: {risk['reason']}\\n\"\n        f\"Auto Paper: {bridge_text}\"\n\n    )\n\n #========================================\n #23.3multiscan\n #==========================================\n\nasync def multiscan(update: Update,\n                    context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n\n    message = (\n        \"\ud83d\udce1 PHASE 5 MULTI SCAN\\n\\n\"\n    )\n\n\n    for coin in PHASE5[\"coins\"]:\n\n\n        result = analyze_coin(\n            coin\n        )\n\n\n        current = result.get(\n            \"price\",\n            0\n        )\n\n\n        score = result.get(\n            \"score\",\n            0\n        )\n\n\n        signal = result.get(\n            \"signal\",\n            \"UNKNOWN\"\n        )\n\n\n        message += (\n\n            f\"{coin}\\n\"\n\n            f\"\u20b9{current}\\n\"\n\n            f\"Score: {score}/100\\n\"\n\n            f\"{signal}\\n\\n\"\n\n        )\n\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n        message\n    )\n\n# =========================================\n# BREAKOUT DETECTOR\n# =========================================\n\nasync def breakout(update: Update,\n                   context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n    if len(context.args) == 0:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"Usage:\\n/breakout sol\"\n        )\n\n        return\n\n    coin = context.args[0].upper()\n\n    if coin not in price_history:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"\u23f3 Collecting market data...\"\n        )\n\n        return\n\n    prices = price_history[coin]\n\n    if len(prices) < 10:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"\u23f3 Need more price history\"\n        )\n\n        return\n\n    current = prices[-1]\n\n    recent_high = max(prices[:-1])\n\n    move = (\n        (current - recent_high)\n        / recent_high\n    ) * 100\n\n    message = (\n        f\"\ud83d\ude80 {coin} BREAKOUT CHECK\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Current: \u20b9{round(current,2)}\\n\"\n        f\"Recent High: \u20b9{round(recent_high,2)}\\n\\n\"\n    )\n\n    if current > recent_high:\n\n        message += (\n            f\"\ud83d\udfe2 Breakout detected\\n\"\n            f\"Strength: {round(move,2)}%\"\n        )\n\n    else:\n\n        message += (\n            \"\u26aa No breakout\"\n        )\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n        message)\n\n\n\n    # ==========================\n    # EMA CHECK\n    # ==========================\n\n    ema_result = analyze_live_ema(\n        coin\n    )\n\n    if \"Bullish\" in ema_result:\n\n        score += 20\n\n        reasons.append(\n            \"EMA bullish \u2705\"\n        )\n\n    elif \"Bearish\" in ema_result:\n\n        score -= 20\n\n        reasons.append(\n            \"EMA bearish \u274c\"\n        )\n\n    else:\n\n        reasons.append(\n            \"EMA collecting data \u23f3\"\n        )\n\n\n    # ==========================\n    # SUPPORT CHECK\n    # ==========================\n\n    if current_price <= support1*1.03:\n\n        score += 15\n\n        reasons.append(\n            \"Near support \u2705\"\n        )\n\n    elif current_price <= support2*1.03:\n\n        score += 10\n\n        reasons.append(\n            \"Near deep support \u2705\"\n        )\n\n    else:\n\n        score -= 5\n\n        reasons.append(\n            \"Far from support \u274c\"\n        )\n\n\n    # ==========================\n    # MOMENTUM CHECK\n    # ==========================\n\n    history = price_history.get(\n        coin,\n        []\n    )\n\n    if len(history) >= 5:\n\n        old_price = history[-5]\n\n        latest_price = history[-1]\n\n        if latest_price > old_price:\n\n            score += 15\n\n            reasons.append(\n                \"Momentum rising \u2705\"\n            )\n\n        elif latest_price < old_price:\n\n            score -= 15\n\n            reasons.append(\n                \"Momentum falling \u274c\"\n            )\n\n    else:\n\n        reasons.append(\n            \"Not enough history \u23f3\"\n        )\n\n\n    # ==========================\n    # VOLATILITY CHECK\n    # ==========================\n\n    if len(history) >= 10:\n\n        high = max(\n            history[-10:]\n        )\n\n        low = min(\n            history[-10:]\n        )\n\n        volatility = (\n            (\n                high-low\n            )\n            /\n            low\n        ) * 100\n\n\n        if volatility > PHASE5[\n            \"alerts\"\n        ][\n            \"volatility_percent\"\n        ]:\n\n            score += 10\n\n            reasons.append(\n                \"Good movement \u2705\"\n            )\n\n        else:\n\n            score -= 5\n\n            reasons.append(\n                \"Low movement \u274c\"\n            )\n\n\n    # LIMIT SCORE\n\n    score = max(\n        0,\n        min(\n            score,\n            100\n        )\n    )\n\n\n    # ==========================\n    # BUILD MESSAGE\n    # ==========================\n\n    message = (\n\n        f\"\ud83d\udd25 {coin} PHASE 5 SCORE\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"Price: \u20b9{round(current_price,2)}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Support 1: \u20b9{round(support1,2)}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Support 2: \u20b9{round(support2,2)}\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"\ud83c\udfaf Score: {score}/100\\n\\n\"\n    )\n\n\n    for item in reasons:\n\n        message += f\"{item}\\n\"\n\n\n    if score >= 80:\n\n        message += (\n            \"\\n\ud83d\udfe2 STRONG BUY\"\n        )\n\n    elif score >= 65:\n\n        message += (\n            \"\\n\ud83d\udfe2 BUY\"\n        )\n\n    elif score >= 45:\n\n        message += (\n            \"\\n\ud83d\udfe1 NEUTRAL\"\n        )\n\n    else:\n\n        message += (\n            \"\\n\ud83d\udd34 WEAK\"\n        )\n\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n        message\n    )\n\n#====================================\n#24 simulate function\n#====================================\n# =========================================\n# SIMULATION\n# =========================================\n\n# =========================================\n# SIMULATION\n# =========================================\n\nasync def simulate(\n    update: Update,\n    context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE\n):\n\n\n    if len(context.args)==0:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n\n            \"Usage:\\n/simulate sol\"\n\n        )\n\n        return\n\n\n    coin = context.args[0].upper()\n\n\n    result = analyze_coin(\n\n        coin\n\n    )\n\n\n    score = result[\n        \"score\"\n    ]\n\n\n    signal = result[\n        \"signal\"\n    ]\n\n\n    current = result[\n        \"price\"\n    ]\n\n\n    capital = get_allocation(\n\n        score\n\n    )\n\n\n    amount = capital[\n        \"amount\"\n    ]\n\n\n    mode = capital[\n        \"mode\"\n    ]\n\n\n    allowed,reason = risk_check(\n\n        score\n\n    )\n\n\n    message=(\n\n        \"\ud83e\udd16 PHASE 5 SIMULATION\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"Coin: {coin}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Current: \u20b9{round(current,2)}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Score: {score}/100\\n\"\n\n        f\"Signal: {signal}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Mode: {mode}\\n\\n\"\n\n    )\n\n\n    if allowed:\n\n        qty = round(\n\n            amount\n            /\n            current,\n\n            6\n\n        )\n\n\n        target = round(\n\n            current\n            *\n            (\n                1\n                +\n                PHASE5[\"trade\"][\"target_percent\"]\n            ),\n\n            2\n\n        )\n\n\n        stop = round(\n\n            current\n            *\n            (\n                1\n                -\n                PHASE5[\"trade\"][\"stoploss_percent\"]\n            ),\n\n            2\n\n        )\n\n\n        message += (\n\n            f\"Capital: \u20b9{amount}\\n\"\n\n            f\"Qty: {qty}\\n\"\n\n            f\"Target: \u20b9{target}\\n\"\n\n            f\"Stop: \u20b9{stop}\\n\\n\"\n\n            f\"{reason}\"\n\n        )\n\n\n    else:\n\n        message += reason\n\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n\n        message\n\n    )\n\n\n\n\n# =========================================\n# DECISION\n# =========================================\n\nasync def decision(\n    update: Update,\n    context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE\n):\n\n\n    if len(context.args)==0:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n\n            \"Usage:\\n/decision sol\"\n\n        )\n\n        return\n\n\n    coin = context.args[0].upper()\n\n\n    result = analyze_coin(\n\n        coin\n\n    )\n\n\n    score = result[\n        \"score\"\n    ]\n\n\n    signal = result[\n        \"signal\"\n    ]\n\n\n    decision_text = \"\"\n\n\n    if score >= 80:\n\n        decision_text = \"\ud83d\udfe2 Strong Buy\"\n\n\n    elif score >= 60:\n\n        decision_text = \"\ud83d\udfe1 Watch / Accumulate\"\n\n\n    else:\n\n        decision_text = \"\ud83d\udd34 Skip\"\n\n\n    message=(\n\n        \"\ud83e\udde0 PHASE 5 DECISION\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"Coin: {coin}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Score: {score}/100\\n\"\n\n        f\"Signal: {signal}\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"Decision:\\n\"\n\n        f\"{decision_text}\"\n\n    )\n\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n\n        message\n\n    )\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n# =========================================\n# MARKET MODE\n# =========================================\n\nasync def market(update, context):\n    # Upgraded to market-wide intelligence in PASS 10.\n    # Delegates to market_cmd() which reads market_intelligence().\n    await market_cmd(update, context)\n# =========================================\n# =========================================\n# CONFIDENCE SCORE\n# =========================================\n\nasync def confidence(\n    update: Update,\n    context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE\n):\n\n    if len(context.args)==0:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n\n            \"Usage:\\n/confidence sol\"\n\n        )\n\n        return\n\n\n    coin = context.args[0].upper()\n\n    result = analyze_coin(\n        coin\n    )\n\n\n    score = result[\n        \"score\"\n    ]\n\n    move = result[\n        \"move\"\n    ]\n\n    price = result[\n        \"price\"\n    ]\n\n    trend = result[\n        \"trend\"\n    ]\n\n\n    confidence = 50\n\n\n    if score >= 70:\n\n        confidence += 20\n\n\n    if price > trend:\n\n        confidence += 15\n\n\n    if move > 1:\n\n        confidence += 15\n\n\n    confidence = min(\n        confidence,\n        100\n    )\n\n\n    level = \"\ud83d\udd34 LOW\"\n\n\n    if confidence >= 80:\n\n        level = \"\ud83d\udfe2 HIGH\"\n\n    elif confidence >= 60:\n\n        level = \"\ud83d\udfe1 MEDIUM\"\n\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n\n        f\"\ud83c\udfaf {coin} CONFIDENCE\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"Confidence: {confidence}%\\n\"\n\n        f\"Level: {level}\"\n\n    )\n# =========================================\n# ENTRY QUALITY\n# =========================================\n\nasync def entry(update: Update,\n                context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n    coin = context.args[0].upper()\n\n    history = price_history.get(\n        coin,\n        []\n    )\n\n    if len(history) < 10:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"\u23f3 Need more market data\"\n        )\n        return\n\n\n    current = history[-1]\n\n    support = min(\n        history[-10:]\n    )\n\n    resistance = max(\n        history[-10:]\n    )\n\n\n    distance = (\n        ((current-support)/support)\n        *100\n    )\n\n\n    if distance < 3:\n\n        quality = \"\ud83d\udfe2 EXCELLENT\"\n\n    elif distance < 8:\n\n        quality = \"\ud83d\udfe1 GOOD\"\n\n    else:\n\n        quality = \"\ud83d\udd34 POOR\"\n\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n\n        f\"\ud83c\udfaf {coin} ENTRY QUALITY\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"Current: \u20b9{round(current,2)}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Support: \u20b9{round(support,2)}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Resistance: \u20b9{round(resistance,2)}\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"Quality: {quality}\"\n    )\n# =========================================\n# FINAL AI DECISION\n# =========================================\n\n# =========================================\n# AI DECISION\n# =========================================\n\nasync def ai(\n    update: Update,\n    context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE\n):\n\n\n    if len(context.args)==0:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n\n            \"Usage:\\n/ai sol\"\n\n        )\n\n        return\n\n\n    coin = context.args[0].upper()\n\n\n    result = analyze_coin(\n\n        coin\n\n    )\n\n\n    score = result[\n        \"score\"\n    ]\n\n    price = result[\n        \"price\"\n    ]\n\n    trend = result[\n        \"trend\"\n    ]\n\n    support1 = result[\n        \"support1\"\n    ]\n\n    move = result[\n        \"move\"\n    ]\n\n\n    reasons=[]\n\n\n    confidence = 50\n\n\n    # Trend\n\n    if price > trend:\n\n        reasons.append(\n\n            \"\u2705 Price above trend\"\n\n        )\n\n        confidence += 10\n\n    else:\n\n        reasons.append(\n\n            \"\u26a0 Price below trend\"\n\n        )\n\n        confidence -= 10\n\n\n    # Momentum\n\n    if move > 1:\n\n        reasons.append(\n\n            \"\u2705 Momentum positive\"\n\n        )\n\n        confidence += 10\n\n    else:\n\n        reasons.append(\n\n            \"\u26a0 Weak momentum\"\n\n        )\n\n\n    # Support\n\n    support_distance = (\n\n        abs(\n            price\n            -\n            support1\n        )\n\n        /\n\n        price\n\n    ) *100\n\n\n    if support_distance < 5:\n\n        reasons.append(\n\n            \"\u2705 Near support zone\"\n\n        )\n\n        confidence += 10\n\n\n    # Score decision\n\n    decision = \"\ud83d\udd34 SKIP\"\n\n\n    if score >= 80:\n\n        decision = \"\ud83d\udfe2 STRONG BUY\"\n\n    elif score >= 60:\n\n        decision = \"\ud83d\udfe1 WATCH / BUY\"\n\n\n    confidence = min(\n        confidence,\n        100\n    )\n\n\n    target = round(\n\n        price\n        *\n        (\n            1\n            +\n            PHASE5[\"trade\"][\"target_percent\"]\n        ),\n\n        2\n\n    )\n\n\n    stop = round(\n\n        price\n        *\n        (\n            1\n            -\n            PHASE5[\"trade\"][\"stoploss_percent\"]\n        ),\n\n        2\n\n    )\n\n\n    message=(\n\n        f\"\ud83e\udd16 {coin} AI DECISION\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"Score: {score}/100\\n\"\n\n        f\"Decision: {decision}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Confidence: {confidence}%\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"Reasons:\\n\\n\"\n\n        + \"\\n\".join(\n            reasons\n        )\n\n        +\n\n        f\"\\n\\nSuggested Entry:\\n\"\n\n        f\"\u20b9{round(price,2)}\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"Target:\\n\"\n\n        f\"\u20b9{target}\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"Stop:\\n\"\n\n        f\"\u20b9{stop}\"\n\n    )\n\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n\n        message\n\n    )\n\n\n# =========================================\n# STRATEGY TEST\n# =========================================\n\nasync def strategy(update: Update,\n                   context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n    if len(context.args) == 0:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"Usage:\\n/strategy sol\"\n        )\n\n        return\n\n\n    coin = context.args[0].upper()\n\n\n    history = price_history.get(\n        coin,\n        []\n    )\n\n\n    if len(history) < 10:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"\u23f3 Need more market data\"\n        )\n\n        return\n\n\n    score = 50\n\n    reasons = []\n\n\n    # Trend\n\n    short_avg = sum(\n        history[-5:]\n    ) / 5\n\n    long_avg = sum(\n        history[-10:]\n    ) / 10\n\n\n    if short_avg > long_avg:\n\n        score += 20\n        reasons.append(\n            \"Trend bullish \u2705\"\n        )\n\n    else:\n\n        score -= 20\n        reasons.append(\n            \"Trend bearish \u274c\"\n        )\n\n\n    # Momentum\n\n    move = (\n\n        (\n            history[-1]\n            -\n            history[-10]\n        )\n\n        /\n\n        history[-10]\n\n    ) * 100\n\n\n    if move > 3:\n\n        score += 20\n        reasons.append(\n            \"Momentum strong \u2705\"\n        )\n\n    else:\n\n        score -= 10\n        reasons.append(\n            \"Momentum weak \u274c\"\n        )\n\n\n    # Support proximity\n\n    support = min(\n        history[-10:]\n    )\n\n    current = history[-1]\n\n\n    distance = (\n        (\n            current-support\n        )\n        /\n        support\n    ) * 100\n\n\n    if distance < 3:\n\n        score += 10\n        reasons.append(\n            \"Near support \u2705\"\n        )\n\n\n    score = max(\n        0,\n        min(\n            score,\n            100\n        )\n    )\n\n\n    if score >= 80:\n\n        signal = \"\ud83d\ude80 STRONG BUY\"\n\n    elif score >= 65:\n\n        signal = \"\ud83d\udfe2 BUY\"\n\n    elif score >= 45:\n\n        signal = \"\ud83d\udfe1 HOLD\"\n\n    else:\n\n        signal = \"\ud83d\udd34 AVOID\"\n\n\n    message = (\n\n        f\"\ud83e\uddea {coin} STRATEGY TEST\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"Score: {score}/100\\n\\n\"\n    )\n\n\n    for         wins\n                /\n\n                total\n\n            )\n\n            *100,\n\n            2\n\n        )\n\n\n    profit = 0\n\n\n    for trade in trade_history:\n\n        pnl =    total,\n\n            2\n\n        )\n\n\n    message=(\n\n        \"\ud83d\udcc8 PERFORMANCE REPORT\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"Trades: {total}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Wins: {wins}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Losses:    await update.message.reply_text(\n        message\n    )\n# =========================================\n# UPDATE PORTFOLIO HISTORY\n# =========================================\n\ndef _position_cost(position):\n    qty = _safe_float(position.get(\"qty\"))\n    avg_cost = _safe_float(position.get(\"avg_cost\", position.get(\"buy_price\")))\n    return _safe_float(position.get(\"amount\"), qty * avg_cost)\n\n\ndef create_grid_plan(coin, entry_price=None, total_amount=None):\n    \"\"\"Create a 4-level Volatile Grid X plan with 7% spacing.\"\"\"\n _safe_float(virtual_balance)\n    )\n\n    if allocation <= 0 or price <= 0:\n        return False, \"Insufficient paper balance\"\n\n    qty = allocation / price\n    position[\"qty\"] =       context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n    global portfolio_history\n\n    if len(portfolio_history) < 2:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"\ud83d\udcc8 Need more =========================================\n# TRAILING STOP\n# =========================================\n\ndef trailing_check(coin):\n\n    if coin not in positions:\n\n        return {\n\n            \"sell\":False\n        }\n\n\n    current = get_cached_price(coin)\n\n\n    position = positions[\n        coin\n    ]\n\n\n    buy = position[\n        \"buy_price\"\n    ]\n\n    # Use .get() so positions loaded from old saves (without \"peak\") don't crash\n    peak = position.get(\"peak\", buy)\n    if \"peak\" not in position:\n        position[\"peak\"] = buy   # backfill for future ticks\n\n\n    profit = (\n\n        (\n            current\n            -\n            buy\n        )\n\n        /\n\n        buy\n\n    ) * 100\n\n\n    # activate after +7%\n\n    if (\n\n        profit >= 7\n\n        and\n\n        not position[\n            \"trailing_active\"\n        ]\n\n    ):\n\n        position[\n            \"trailing_active\"\n        ] = True\n\n\n    # update peak\n\n    if current > peak:\n\n        position[\n            \"peak\"\n        ] = current\n\n        peak = current\n\n\n    # trailing exit 5% from peak\n\n    if position[\n        \"trailing_active\"\n    ]:\n\n        drop = (\n\n            (\n\n                peak\n                -\n                current\n\n            )\n\n            /\n\n            peak\n\n        ) *100\n\n\n        if drop >= 5:\n\n            return {\n\n                \"sell\":True,\n                \"reason\":\"Trailing Stop\"\n\n            }\n\n\n    return {\n\n        \"sell\":False\n    }\n#===================================\n#25 auto function\n#==================================\n# 25.1 auto alerts\n # =========================================\n# SMART AUTO ALERTS\n# =========================================\n\nlast_alerts = {}\n\nasync def auto_alerts(app):\n\n    global CHAT_ID\n\n    if CHAT_ID is None:\n        return\n\n    watchlist = [\n        \"BTC\",\n        \"SOL\",\n        \"ETH\",\n        \"ZEC\"\n    ]\n\n    import time\n\n    message = \"\ud83d\udea8 PHASE 5 ALERTS\\n\\n\"\n\n    alert_found = False\n\n    for coin in watchlist:\n\n SOL Buy Zone\n        if coin == \"SOL\" and current_price < 9000:\n\n            key = f\"{coin}_buy\"\n\n            if (\n                key not in last_alerts\n                or time.time()-last_alerts[key] > 1800\n            ):\n\n                message += (\n                    f\"\ud83d\udfe2 SOL BUY ZONE\\n\"\n                    f\"Price: \u20b9{round(current_price,2)}\\n\\n\"\n                )\n\n                last_alerts[key] = time.time()\n\n                alert_found = True\n\n\n        # EMA signal\n        ema_result = analyze_live_ema(\n            coin\n        )\n\n        if \"Bullish\" in ema_result:\n\n            key = f\"{coin}_bull\"\n\n            if (\n                key not in last_alerts\n                or time.time()-last_alerts[key] > 1800\n            ):\n\n                message += (\n                    f\"\ud83d\udcc8 {coin} Bullish EMA\\n\\n\"\n                )\n\n                last_alerts[key] = time.time()\n\n                alert_found = True\n\n\n    # SEND ONLY IF SOMETHING CHANGED\n    if alert_found:\n\n        await app.bot.send_message(\n            chat_id=CHAT_ID,\n            text=message\n        )\n# =========================================\n# PRICE ALERTS\n# =========================================\n\nprice_alerts = {}\n\n\nasync def set_alert(update: Update,\n                    context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):\n\n\n    if len(context.args) < 2:\n\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n\n            \"Usage:\\n/alert sol 8500\"\n\n        )\n\n        return\n\n\n    coin = context.args[0].upper()\n\n    target = float(\n        context.args[1]\n    )\n\n\n    price_alerts[\n        coin\n    ] = target\n\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n\n        f\"\ud83d\udd14 Alert set\\n\\n\"\n\n        f\"{coin}\\n\"\n\n        f\"Target: \u20b9{target}\"\n\n    )\n\n # =========================================\n# 25.4 background_loop\n# =========================================\nasync def background_loop():\n\n    global virtual_balance\n\n    loop_state[\"running\"]    = True\n    loop_state[\"started_at\"] = datetime.now().strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\")\n\n    while True:\n\n        try:\n\n            loop_state[\"tick_count\"] += 1\n            loop_state[\"last_tick\"]   = datetime.now().strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\")\n\n            # =========================\n            # REFRESH MARKET CACHE\n            # (single API call for all coins)\n            # =========================\n\n            update_market_cache()\n\n            # =========================\n            # REFRESH MARKET INTELLIGENCE\n            # (runs after cache so prices are fresh)\n            # =========================\n\n            market_intelligence()\n\n            # =========================\n            # FEED PRICE HISTORY FROM CACHE\n            # =========================\n\n            for coin in PHASE5[\"coins\"]:\n\n                current = get_cached_price(coin)\n\n                if current > 0:\n\n                    if coin not in price_history:\n\n                        price_history[\n                            coin\n                        ] = []\n\n\n                    price_history[\n                        coin\n                    ].append(\n                        current\n                    )\n\n\n                    price_history[\n                        coin\n                    ] = (\n\n                        price_history[\n                            coin\n                        ][-100:]\n\n                    )\n\n\n            # =========================\n            # AUTO SCALE OUT\n            # =========================\n\n            for coin in list(\n                positions.keys()\n            ):\n\n                grid_action = manage_grid_position(coin)\n                if grid_action.get(\"managed\"):\n                    print(\n                        f\"{coin} GRID: {grid_action['reason']}\"\n                    )\n\n                if coin not in positions:\n                    continue\n\n                current = get_cached_price(coin)\n\n                buy_price = positions[\n                    coin\n                ][\n                    \"buy_price\"\n                ]\n\n\n                move = (\n\n                    (\n                        current\n                        -\n                        buy_price\n                    )\n\n                    /\n\n                    buy_price\n\n                )\n\n\n                if move >= TAKE_PROFIT[3]:\n\n                    print(\n                        f\"{coin}: TP3 reached\"\n                    )\n\n\n                elif move >= TAKE_PROFIT[2]:\n\n                    print(\n                        f\"{coin}: TP2 reached\"\n                    )\n\n\n                elif move >= TAKE_PROFIT[1]:\n\n                    print(\n                        f\"{coin}: TP1 reached\"\n                    )\n\n\n            # =========================\n            # EXIT CHECK\n            # =========================\n\n            for coin in list(positions.keys()):\n\n                risk = exit_check(coin)\n\n                trail = trailing_check(coin)\n\n                if trail[\"sell\"]:\n\n                    risk = {\n                        \"exit\":True,\n                        \"reason\":trail[\"reason\"]\n                    }\n\n\n                if risk[\"exit\"]:\n\n                    current = get_cached_price(coin)\n\n                    qty = positions[coin][\"qty\"]\n\n                    value = current * qty\n\n                    pnl = (\n                        value\n                        -\n                        positions[coin][\"amount\"]\n                    )\n\n\n                    virtual_balance += value\n\n\n                    log_trade(\n                        coin,\n                        \"AUTO SELL\",\n                        current,\n                        value,\n                        pnl\n                    )\n\n\n                    update_stats(\n                        \"SELL\",\n                        pnl\n                    )\n\n\n                    print(\n                        f\"{coin} AUTO SELL: {risk['reason']}\"\n                    )\n\n\n                    del positions[coin]\n\n                    save_data()\n\n\n                    print(\n                        f\"{coin} exited\"\n                    )\n\n\n            # =========================\n            # PERIODIC DRIVE SYNC\n            # =========================\n\n            _maybe_periodic_sync()\n\n\n            await auto_alerts(\n                app\n            )\n\n\n            await asyncio.sleep(\n                30\n            )\n\n\n        except Exception as e:\n\n            loop_state[\"error_count\"] += 1\n            loop_state[\"last_error\"]   = datetime.now().strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\")\n\n            _record_error(\n                \"background_loop\",\n                e,\n                _traceback.format_exc()\n            )\n\n            print(\n                f\"Loop Error: {e}\"\n            )\n\n            await asyncio.sleep(\n                10\n            )\n\n\n# =========================================\n# MARKET CACHE ENGINE  (PASS 6)\n# =========================================\n\nmarket_state = {\n    \"prices\": {},        # coin \u2192 price (INR)\n    \"timestamp\": 0,      # epoch seconds of last full update\n    \"source\": \"none\",    # \"live\" | \"fallback\" | \"none\"\n    \"update_count\": 0,   # total successful refreshes\n    \"usdt_inr\": 86.0,    # live USDT/INR rate, updated by update_market_cache()\n}\n\nCACHE_TTL = 30          # seconds before cache is considered stale\n\n\ndef update_market_cache():\n    \"\"\"\n    Fetch all tickers once and store every watchlist coin price.\n    Returns True on success, False on failure.\n    Designed to be called from background_loop so callers never\n    need to hit the API directly for a simple price read.\n    \"\"\"\n    global market_state\n\n    try:\n        url = \"https://api.coindcx.com/exchange/ticker\"\n        response = requests.get(url, timeout=10)\n        response.raise_for_status()\n        data = response.json()\n\n        # Build a fast lookup: market_name \u2192 price\n        # CoinDCX records can use last_price, lastPrice, or price.\n        # Records missing both market and a valid price are skipped safely.\n        ticker_map = {}\n        for item in data:\n            market = item.get(\"market\") or item.get(\"symbol\")\n            if not market:\n                continue\n            raw = (\n                item.get(\"last_price\")\n                or item.get(\"lastPrice\")\n                or item.get(\"price\")\n            )\n            if raw is None:\n                continue\n            try:\n                ticker_map[market] = float(raw)\n            except (ValueError, TypeError):\n                continue\n\n        # Resolve USDT\u2192INR rate once and store it for _get_usdt_inr_rate()\n        usdt_inr = ticker_map.get(\"USDTINR\", 86.0)\n        market_state[\"usdt_inr\"] = usdt_inr   # used by fetch_historical_data\n\n        new_prices = {}\n        for coin in PHASE5[\"coins\"]:\n            coin_upper = coin.upper()\n            inr_pair   = coin_upper + \"INR\"\n            usdt_pair  = coin_upper + \"USDT\"\n\n            if inr_pair in ticker_map:\n                new_prices[coin_upper] = round(ticker_map[inr_pair], 2)\n\n            elif usdt_pair in ticker_map:\n                new_prices[coin_upper] = round(\n                    ticker_map[usdt_pair] * usdt_inr, 2\n                )\n            # else coin stays missing \u2014 safe to skip\n\n        market_state[\"prices\"]       = new_prices\n        market_state[\"timestamp\"]    = time.time()\n        market_state[\"source\"]       = \"live\"\n        market_state[\"update_count\"] += 1\n\n        return True\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"[Cache] update failed: {e}\")\n        market_state[\"source\"] = \"fallback\"\n        return False\n\n\ndef get_cached_price(coin):\n    \"\"\"\n    Return cached INR price for coin, or 0 if missing.\n    No network call.  Fast path used by analysis functions.\n    \"\"\"\n    return market_state[\"prices\"].get(coin.upper(), 0)\n\n\ndef get_cached_price_safe(coin):\n    \"\"\"\n    Return cached price if cache is fresh (< CACHE_TTL seconds old).\n    Falls back to a live get_price() call if cache is stale or missing.\n    \"\"\"\n    age = time.time() - market_state[\"timestamp\"]\n    cached = market_state[\"prices\"].get(coin.upper(), 0)\n\n    if cached > 0 and age < CACHE_TTL:\n        return cached\n\n    # Cache stale or coin missing \u2192 live fallback\n    live = get_price(coin)\n    if live > 0:\n        market_state[\"prices\"][coin.upper()] = live  # patch cache entry\n    return live\n\n\ndef cache_health():\n    \"\"\"\n    Return a dict describing the current cache state.\n    Used by /cachestatus command and internal diagnostics.\n    \"\"\"\n    now      = time.time()\n    age      = now - market_state[\"timestamp\"]\n    is_fresh = age < CACHE_TTL and len(market_state[\"prices\"]) > 0\n    missing  = [\n        c for c in PHASE5[\"coins\"]\n        if c.upper() not in market_state[\"prices\"]\n    ]\n\n    return {\n        \"coins_cached\": len(market_state[\"prices\"]),\n        \"prices\":       market_state[\"prices\"],\n        \"age_seconds\":  round(age, 1),\n        \"fresh\":        is_fresh,\n        \"source\":       market_state[\"source\"],\n        \"update_count\": market_state[\"update_count\"],\n        \"missing\":      missing,\n    }\n\n\ndef cache_status():\n    \"\"\"\n    Return a human-readable cache status string for the /cachestatus command.\n    \"\"\"\n    health = cache_health()\n    age    = health[\"age_seconds\"]\n\n    if age < 60:\n        age_str = f\"{int(age)} sec ago\"\n    else:\n        age_str = f\"{int(age // 60)} min ago\"\n\n    coin_lines = \"\"\n    for coin in PHASE5[\"coins\"]:\n        p = health[\"prices\"].get(coin.upper(), 0)\n        status = \"OK\" if p > 0 else \"MISSING\"\n        coin_lines += f\"{coin}: {status}  \"\n\n    healthy_label = \"YES \u2705\" if health[\"fresh\"] else \"NO \u274c (stale)\"\n\n    return (\n        f\"\ud83d\udce1 CACHE STATUS\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Coins Cached: {health['coins_cached']}\\n\"\n        f\"{coin_lines.strip()}\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Last Update: {age_str}\\n\"\n        f\"Source: {health['source']}\\n\"\n        f\"Updates Done: {health['update_count']}\\n\"\n        f\"Healthy: {healthy_label}\"\n    )\n\n\n# =========================================\n# /cachestatus COMMAND\n# =========================================\n\nasync def cachestatus(update, context):\n    await update.message.reply_text(cache_status())\n\n\n# =========================================\n# /cachehealth COMMAND  (PASS 7)\n# =========================================\n\nasync def cachehealth_cmd(update, context):\n    ch  = cache_health()\n    age = ch[\"age_seconds\"]\n\n    if age < 60:\n        age_str = f\"{int(age)} sec ago\"\n    elif age < 3600:\n        age_str = f\"{int(age // 60)} min ago\"\n    else:\n        age_str = f\"{int(age // 3600)}h ago\"\n\n    fresh_label =       status = f\"\u20b9{p:,.2f}\" if p > 0 else \"MISSING \u274c\"\n        coin_rows.append(f\"  {coin.upper():<6} {status}\")\n\n    missing_str = (\n        \", \".join(ch[\"missing\"]) if (PASS 7)\n# =========================================\n\nasync def storagehealth_cmd(update, context):\n    sh = storage_health()\n\n    local_label  = \"OK \u2705\"    if sh[\"local_ok\"]  else =========================================\n# /storage COMMAND  (PASS 9)\n# =========================================\n\nasync def storage_cmd(update, context):\n    h = storage_health()\n\n \u274c\"\n    )\n    backup_label = \"AVAILABLE \u2705\" if h[\"backup_available\"] else \"NONE \u26a0\ufe0f\"\n\n    # Overall status\n    if not h[\"local_ok\"] and (h[\"drive_enabled\"] and not status = \"HEALTHY \u2705\"\n\n    msg = (\n        f\"\ud83d\udcbe {PROJECT_NAME} STORAGE\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Local JSON:      {local_label}\\n\"\n        f\"Drive Sync:      {drive_label}\\n\"\n {h['last_restore']}\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Positions:       {h['positions']}\\n\"\n        f\"Trade Records:   {h['trade_records']}\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Saves Done:      {h['save_count']}\\n\"\n        f\"Drive Syncs:     {h['drive_sync_count']}\\n\"\n        f\"Storage Errors:  {h['storage_errors']}\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Status: {status}\"\n    )\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(msg)\n\n# =========================================\n# PRODUCTION STABILITY LAYER  (PASS 8)\n# =========================================\n\nimport traceback as _traceback\n\n# Runtime error log \u2014 never written to disk (transient)\nerror_log = []          # list of {time, command, error, trace}\nerror_counts = {}       # command/source \u2192 count\n\nMAX_ERROR_LOG = 100     # keep last N errors in memory\n\n# Background loop telemetry\nloop_state = {\n    \"running\":       False,\n    \"tick_count\":    0,\n    \"error_count\":   0,\n    \"last_tick\":     None,    # datetime string\n    \"started_at\":    None,    # datetime string\n    \"last_error\":    None,    # datetime string of most recent loop error\n}\n\n\ndef _record_error(source, error, trace=\"\"):\n    \"\"\"Append to in-memory error_log. Caps at MAX_ERROR_LOG entries.\"\"\"\n    exc_type = type(error).__name__ if error is not None else \"Unknown\"\n    entry = {\n        \"time\":    datetime.now().strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\"),\n        \"source\":  source,\n        \"type\":    exc_type,\n        \"error\":   str(error),\n        \"trace\":   trace,\n    }\n    error_log.append(entry)\n    if len(error_log) > MAX_ERROR_LOG:\n        del error_log[0]\n\n    error_counts[source] = error_counts.get(source, 0) + 1\n    print(f\"[ERROR] {entry['time']} | {source} | {exc_type}: {error}\")\n\n\nasync def error_handler(update, context):\n    \"\"\"\n    Global PTB error handler.\n    Registered via app.add_error_handler(error_handler).\n    Catches all unhandled exceptions from command handlers.\n    \"\"\"\n    source = \"unknown\"\n    try:\n        if update and update.effective_message:\n            # Extract command name from message text\n            text = update.effective_message.text or \"\"\n            source = text.split()[0] if text.startswith(\"/\") else \"handler\"\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n\n    error  = context.error\n    trace  = _traceback.format_exc()\n    _record_error(source, error, trace)\n\n    # Notify user that something went wrong (non-crashing)\n    try:\n        if update and update.effective_message:\n            await update.effective_message.reply_text(\n                f\"\u26a0\ufe0f Command error\\n\\n\"\n                f\"Source: {source}\\n\"\n                f\"Error: {str(error)[:200]}\\n\\n\"\n                f\"The bot remains running.\"\n            )\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n\n\n# \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n# SUBSYSTEM HEALTH CHECKS\n# _check_trading():\n    \"\"\"Trading OK if positions dict accessible and virtual_balance is a number.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        assert isinstance(positions, dict)\n        assert isinstance(virtual_balance, (int, float))\n        return True\n    except Exception:\n        return False\n\n\ndef _check_ai():\n    \"\"\"AI OK if analyze_coin is callable and returns expected keys.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        result = analyze_coin(\"BTC\")\n        assert \"score\" in result and \"signal\" in result\n        return True\n    except Exception:\n        return False\n\n\ndef _check_analytics():\n    \"\"\"Analytics OK if trade_log and trade_stats are accessible.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        assert isinstance(trade_log, list)\n        assert isinstance(trade_stats, dict)\n        assert \"wins\" in trade_stats\n        return True\n    except Exception:\n        return False\n\n\ndef _check_storage():\n    \"\"\"Storage OK if at least one of local/drive has saved successfully.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        h = storage_health()\n        return h[\"local_ok\"] or h[\"drive_ok\"]\n    except Exception:\n        return False\n\n\ndef _check_cache():\n    \"\"\"Cache OK if market_state has a recent timestamp and at least one price.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        age = time.time() - market_state[\"timestamp\"]\n        return len(market_state[\"prices\"]) > 0 and age < 300\n    except Exception:\n        return False\n\n\ndef trading_health():\n    \"\"\"\n    Return a dict describing the trading engine state.\n    Includes positions, balance, open PnL, and loop telemetry.\n    \"\"\"\n    try:\n        unrealised_pnl = 0.0\n        position_details = []\n        for coin, pos in positions.items():\n            entry  = float(pos.get(\"entry_price\", 0))\n            qty    = float(pos.get(\"amount\", 0))\n            cached = get_cached_price(coin)\n            live   = cached if cached > 0 else entry\n            pnl    = (live - entry) * qty if entry > 0 else 0.0\n            unrealised_pnl += pnl\n            position_details.append({\n                \"coin\":    coin,\n                \"entry\":   entry,\n                \"current\": live,\n                \"pnl\":     round(pnl, 2),\n                \"qty\":     qty,\n            })\n\n        return {\n            \"virtual_balance\":  round(virtual_balance, 2),\n            \"open_positions\":   len(positions),\n            \"positions\":        position_details,\n            \"unrealised_pnl\":   round(unrealised_pnl, 2),\n            \"total_trades\":     trade_stats.get(\"total_trades\", 0),\n            \"wins\":             trade_stats.get(\"wins\", 0),\n            \"losses\":           trade_stats.get(\"losses\", 0),\n            \"total_profit\":     round(trade_stats.get(\"total_profit\", 0.0), 2),\n            \"loop_running\":     loop_state[\"running\"],\n            \"loop_tick\":        loop_state[\"tick_count\"],\n            \"loop_last_tick\":   loop_state[\"last_tick\"] or \"never\",\n            \"loop_started\":     loop_state[\"started_at\"] or \"never\",\n            \"loop_errors\":      loop_state[\"error_count\"],\n        }\n    except Exception as e:\n        return {\"error\": str(e)}\n\n\ndef system_health():\n    \"\"\"\n    Run all subsystem checks.\n    Returns a dict with per-subsystem status and an overall healthy flag.\n    \"\"\"\n    checks = {\n        \"Trading\":   _check_trading(),\n        \"AI\":        _check_ai(),\n        \"Analytics\": _check_analytics(),\n        \"Storage\":   _check_storage(),\n        \"Cache\":     _check_cache(),\n        \"Loop\":      loop_state[\"running\"],\n    }\n    total_errors = sum(error_counts.values())\n    all_ok       = all(checks.values())\n\n    return {\n        \"checks\":         checks,\n        \"total_errors\":   total_errors,\n        \"healthy\":        all_ok,\n        \"loop_last_tick\": loop_state[\"last_tick\"] or \"never\",\n        \"open_positions\": len(positions),\n    }\n\n\n# \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n# /health COMMAND\n# def health_cmd(update, context):\n    h  = system_health()\n    th = trading_health()\n\n    lines = [\"\ud83e\ude7a SYSTEM HEALTH\\n\"]\n\n    # Subsystem rows\n    for name, ok in lines.append(f\"Last Loop Tick: {h['loop_last_tick']}\")\n    lines.append(f\"Total Errors:   {h['total_errors']}\")\n    lines.append(\"\")\n    lines.append(f\"Status: {'HEALTHY \u2705' if h['healthy'] else 'DEGRADED \u26a0\ufe0f'}\")\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\"\\n\".join(lines))\n\n\n# \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n#
