def errors_cmd(update, context):\n    if not error_log:\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"\u2705 ERROR LOG\\n\\nNo errors recorded.\"\n        )\n        return\n\n    total = sum(error_counts.values())\n    lines = [f\"\ud83d\udea8 ERROR LOG  (total: {total})\\n\"]\n\n    # Show last 5 errors most-recent-first\n    for entry in reversed(error_log[-5:]):\n        lines.append(\n            f\"\u23f1 {entry['time']}\\n\"\n            f\"Source: {entry['source']}\\n\"\n            f\"Error: {entry['error'][:120]}\\n\"\n        )\n\n    lines.append(\"\\nTop error sources:\")\n    for src, count in sorted(error_counts.items(),\n                             key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:5]:\n        lines.append(f\"  {src}: {count}x\")\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\"\\n\".join(lines))\n\n\n# =========================================\n# STRATEGY ANALYTICS LAYER  (PASS 9A)\n# =========================================\n\ndef strategy_summary():\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute full strategy performance summary from trade_log.\n    trade_log entries: {coin, action, price, amount, pnl, time}\n    Returns a dict safe to call with an empty trade_log.\n    \"\"\"\n    sells = [t for t in trade_log if t.get(\"action\") in (\"SELL\", \"AUTO SELL\")]\n\n    if not sells:\n        return {\n            \"total_trades\":    0,\n            \"wins\":            0,\n            \"losses\":          0,\n            \"win_rate\":        0.0,\n            \"total_profit\":    0.0,\n            \"avg_win\":         0.0,\n            \"avg_loss\":        0.0,\n            \"best_trade\":      0.0,\n            \"worst_trade\":     0.0,\n            \"current_drawdown\":0.0,\n            \"profit_factor\":   0.0,\n        }\n\n    win_pnls  = [t[\"pnl\"] for t in sells if t.get(\"pnl\", 0) > 0]\n    loss_pnls = [t[\"pnl\"] for t in sells if t.get(\"pnl\", 0) <= 0]\n\n    total   = len(sells)\n    wins    = len(win_pnls)\n    losses  = len(loss_pnls)\n    win_rate = round(wins / total * 100, 2) if total else 0.0\n\n    total_profit = round(sum(t.get(\"pnl\", 0) for t in sells), 2)\n    avg_win      = round(sum(win_pnls)  / wins   if wins   else 0.0, 2)\n    avg_loss     = round(sum(loss_pnls) / losses if losses else 0.0, 2)\n    best_trade   = round(max(win_pnls)  if win_pnls  else 0.0, 2)\n    worst_trade  = round(min(loss_pnls) if loss_pnls else 0.0, 2)\n\n    gross_profit = sum(win_pnls)\n    gross_loss   = abs(sum(loss_pnls))\n    profit_factor = round(gross_profit / gross_loss if gross_loss > 0 else float(\"inf\"), 2)\n\n    # Current drawdown from portfolio_history\n    current_dd = 0.0\n    if len(portfolio_history) >= 2:\n        values = [p[\"portfolio\"] for p in portfolio_history]\n        peak   = max(values)\n        latest = values[-1]\n        if peak > 0:\n            current_dd = round((peak - latest) / peak * 100, 2)\n\n    return {\n        \"total_trades\":     total,\n        \"wins\":             wins,\n        \"losses\":           losses,\n        \"win_rate\":         win_rate,\n        \"total_profit\":     total_profit,\n        \"avg_win\":          avg_win,\n        \"avg_loss\":         avg_loss,\n        \"best_trade\":       best_trade,\n        \"worst_trade\":      worst_trade,\n        \"current_drawdown\": current_dd,\n        \"profit_factor\":    profit_factor,\n    }\n\n\ndef leaderboard():\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute per-coin performance from trade_log.\n    Returns a list of dicts sorted by total_pnl descending.\n    Handles empty trade_log safely.\n    \"\"\"\n    sells = [t for t in trade_log if t.get(\"action\") in (\"SELL\", \"AUTO SELL\")]\n\n    coin_data = {}\n    for t in sells:\n        coin = t.get(\"coin\", \"UNKNOWN\")\n        pnl  = t.get(\"pnl\", 0)\n        if coin not in coin_data:\n            coin_data[coin] = {\"trades\": 0, \"wins\": 0, \"losses\": 0, \"total_pnl\": 0.0}\n        coin_data[coin][\"trades\"]    += 1\n        coin_data[coin][\"total_pnl\"] += pnl\n        if pnl > 0:\n            coin_data[coin][\"wins\"]   += 1\n        else:\n            coin_data[coin][\"losses\"] += 1\n\n    result = []\n    for coin, d in coin_data.items():\n        win_rate = round(d[\"wins\"] / d[\"trades\"] * 100, 1) if d[\"trades\"] else 0.0\n        result.append({\n            \"coin\":      coin,\n            \"trades\":    d[\"trades\"],\n            \"wins\":      d[\"wins\"],\n            \"losses\":    d[\"losses\"],\n            \"win_rate\":  win_rate,\n            \"total_pnl\": round(d[\"total_pnl\"], 2),\n        })\n\n    result.sort(key=lambda x: x[\"total_pnl\"], reverse=True)\n    return result\n\n\ndef scorecard():\n    \"\"\"\n    AI signal accuracy scorecard derived from trade_log.\n    Counts BUY signals issued, resulting SELL outcomes, and accuracy rate.\n    Handles empty trade_log safely.\n    \"\"\"\n    buys      = [t for t in trade_log if t.get(\"action\") in (\"buy\", \"BUY\")]\n    sells     = [t for t in trade_log if t.get(\"action\") in (\"SELL\", \"AUTO SELL\")]\n    wins      = [t for t in sells if t.get(\"pnl\", 0) > 0]\n    losses    = [t for t in sells if t.get(\"pnl\", 0) <= 0]\n    completed = len(sells)\n    accuracy  = round(len(wins) / completed * 100, 1) if completed else 0.0\n\n    return {\n        \"buy_signals\":       len(buys),\n        \"sell_signals\":      completed,\n        \"successful_trades\": len(wins),\n        \"failed_trades\":     len(losses),\n        \"accuracy\":          accuracy,\n        \"open_positions\":    len(positions),\n        \"total_log_entries\": len(trade_log),\n    }\n\n\n# =========================================\n# /strategy COMMAND  (PASS 9A)\n# =========================================\n\nasync def strategy_report(update, context):\n    s = strategy_summary()\n\n    if s[\"total_trades\"] == 0:\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"\ud83d\udcca STRATEGY SUMMARY\\n\\n\"\n            \"No completed trades yet.\\n\"\n            \"Use /buy and /sell to start tracking.\"\n        )\n        return\n\n    pf_str = (\n        f\"{s['profit_factor']}\"\n        if s[\"profit_factor\"] != float(\"inf\")\n        else \"\u221e (no losses)\"\n    )\n\n    dd_label = \"\ud83d\udfe2 Healthy\"\n    if s[\"current_drawdown\"] >= 15:\n        dd_label = \"\ud83d\udd34 High Risk\"\n    elif s[\"current_drawdown\"] >= 5:\n        dd_label = \"\ud83d\udfe1 Moderate\"\n\n    msg = (\n        f\"\ud83d\udcca STRATEGY SUMMARY\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Total Trades:     {s['total_trades']}\\n\"\n        f\"Wins:             {s['wins']}\\n\"\n        f\"Losses:           {s['losses']}\\n\"\n        f\"Win Rate:         {s['win_rate']}%\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Total Profit:     \u20b9{s['total_profit']}\\n\"\n        f\"Avg Win:          \u20b9{s['avg_win']}\\n\"\n        f\"Avg Loss:         \u20b9{s['avg_loss']}\\n\"\n        f\"Best Trade:       \u20b9{s['best_trade']}\\n\"\n        f\"Worst Trade:      \u20b9{s['worst_trade']}\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Profit Factor:    {pf_str}\\n\"\n        f\"Current Drawdown: {s['current_drawdown']}%  {dd_label}\"\n    )\n    await update.message.reply_text(msg)\n\n\n# =========================================\n# /leaderboard COMMAND  (PASS 9A)\n# =========================================\n\nasync def leaderboard_cmd(update, context):\n    board = leaderboard()\n\n    if not board:\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"\ud83c\udfc6 LEADERBOARD\\n\\n\"\n            \"No completed trades yet.\"\n        )\n        return\n\n    best  = board[0]\n    worst = board[-1]\n\n    lines = [\"\ud83c\udfc6 COIN LEADERBOARD\\n\"]\n\n    for i, row in enumerate(board, 1):\n        medal = \"\ud83e\udd47\" if i == 1 else (\"\ud83e\udd48\" if i == 2 else (\"\ud83e\udd49\" if i == 3 else f\"{i}.\"))\n        pnl_icon = \"\ud83d\udfe2\" if row[\"total_pnl\"] >= 0 else \"\ud83d\udd34\"\n        lines.append(\n            f\"{medal} {row['coin']}\\n\"\n            f\"   PnL: {pnl_icon} \u20b9{row['total_pnl']}\\n\"\n            f\"   Trades: {row['trades']}  |  \"\n            f\"Win Rate: {row['win_rate']}%\\n\"\n        )\n\n    lines.append(f\"\\nBest Coin:  {best['coin']} (\u20b9{best['total_pnl']})\")\n    lines.append(f\"Worst Coin: {worst['coin']} (\u20b9{worst['total_pnl']})\")\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\"\\n\".join(lines))\n\n\n# =========================================\n# /scorecard COMMAND  (PASS 9A)\n# =========================================\n\nasync def scorecard_cmd(update, context):\n    sc = scorecard()\n\n    accuracy_label = \"\ud83d\udd34 Poor\"\n    if sc[\"accuracy\"] >= 60:\n        accuracy_label = \"\ud83d\udfe2 Good\"\n    elif sc[\"accuracy\"] >= 45:\n        accuracy_label = \"\ud83d\udfe1 Fair\"\n\n    msg = (\n        f\"\ud83c\udfaf AI SCORECARD\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Buy Signals:       {sc['buy_signals']}\\n\"\n        f\"Sell Signals:      {sc['sell_signals']}\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Successful Trades: {sc['successful_trades']}\\n\"\n        f\"Failed Trades:     {sc['failed_trades']}\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Accuracy:          {sc['accuracy']}%  {accuracy_label}\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Open Positions:    {sc['open_positions']}\\n\"\n        f\"Total Log Entries: {sc['total_log_entries']}\"\n    )\n    await update.message.reply_text(msg)\n\n\n# =========================================\n# BACKTESTING ENGINE V1  (PASS 9B)\n# =========================================\n# All functions operate on COPIES of data.\n# Live state (positions, trade_log, virtual_balance) is NEVER mutated.\n\n# Minimum candles needed to generate a valid backtest signal\nBACKTEST_MIN_HISTORY = 10\n\n# Simulate the Phase 5 scoring logic on a price window slice.\n# This mirrors analyze_coin() but is fed a window list directly,\n# so it never reads the live price_history global.\ndef _bt_score(window):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute a Phase 5 score for a price window (list of floats).\n    Returns (score, signal_str).  Requires len(window) >= 3.\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(window) < 3:\n        return 20, \"SKIP\"\n\n    current   = window[-1]\n    short_avg = sum(window[-min(5, len(window)):]) / min(5, len(window))\n    long_avg  = sum(window) / len(window)\n    first     = window[0]\n    move      = ((current - first) / first) * 100 if first > 0 else 0\n\n    score = 50\n    if short_avg > long_avg:\n        score += 20\n    if move > 3:\n        score += 15\n    if move > 7:\n        score += 10\n    score = min(score, 100)\n\n    if score >= 85:\n        signal = \"STRONG BUY\"\n    elif score >= 70:\n        signal = \"BUY\"\n    elif score >= 60:\n        signal = \"WATCH\"\n    else:\n        signal = \"SKIP\"\n\n    return score, signal\n\n\ndef simulate_trade(entry_price, prices_after_entry, target_pct, stop_pct):\n    \"\"\"\n    Simulate a single trade given an entry price and the price series\n    that follows it.\n\n    Rules (mirrors live trailing logic):\n      - Exit at first candle that hits target_pct gain \u2192 WIN\n      - Exit at first candle that hits stop_pct loss  \u2192 LOSS\n      - If neither hit, close at the last candle      \u2192 WIN/LOSS by final price\n\n    Returns dict: {outcome, pnl_pct, exit_price, candles_held}\n    All inputs are floats; no global state is read or written.\n    \"\"\"\n    target_price = entry_price * (1 + target_pct)\n    stop_price   = entry_price * (1 - stop_pct)\n\n    for i, price in enumerate(prices_after_entry):\n        if price >= target_price:\n            pnl_pct = (price - entry_price) / entry_price * 100\n            return {\"outcome\": \"WIN\", \"pnl_pct\": round(pnl_pct, 3),\n                    \"exit_price\": price, \"candles_held\": i + 1}\n        if price <= stop_price:\n            pnl_pct = (price - entry_price) / entry_price * 100\n            return {\"outcome\": \"LOSS\", \"pnl_pct\": round(pnl_pct, 3),\n                    \"exit_price\": price, \"candles_held\": i + 1}\n\n    # Time-exit: close at last available candle\n    last_price = prices_after_entry[-1] if prices_after_entry else entry_price\n    pnl_pct = (last_price - entry_price) / entry_price * 100\n    outcome = \"WIN\" if pnl_pct > 0 else \"LOSS\"\n    return {\"outcome\": outcome, \"pnl_pct\": round(pnl_pct, 3),\n            \"exit_price\": last_price, \"candles_held\": len(prices_after_entry)}\n\n\ndef backtest_coin(coin, buy_threshold=70, min_history=BACKTEST_MIN_HISTORY):\n    \"\"\"\n    Replay the stored price_history for 'coin' using Phase 5 signal logic.\n    Enters a trade whenever the rolling score crosses buy_threshold.\n    Exits via simulate_trade() (target 7%, stop 3%) \u2014 matching PHASE5 config.\n    One trade at a time; no overlapping positions.\n\n    Returns a result dict safe to display even with thin history:\n      {\"coin\", \"candles\", \"trades\", \"wins\", \"losses\", \"win_rate\",\n       \"total_pnl_pct\", \"avg_win_pct\", \"avg_loss_pct\", \"profit_factor\",\n       \"max_drawdown_pct\", \"result_label\", \"enough_data\"}\n    \"\"\"\n    # Work on a COPY \u2014 never touch the live global\n    history = list(price_history.get(coin, []))\n\n    target_pct = PHASE5[\"trade\"][\"target_percent\"]   # 0.07\n    stop_pct   = PHASE5[\"trade\"][\"stoploss_percent\"]  # 0.03\n\n    empty_result = {\n        \"coin\": coin, \"candles\": len(history),\n        \"trades\": 0, \"total\": 0,              # \"total\" alias consumed by report / backtest_full\n        \"wins\": 0, \"losses\": 0,\n        \"win_rate\": 0.0, \"total_pnl_pct\": 0.0, \"net_pnl\": 0.0,\n        \"avg_win_pct\": 0.0,  \"avg_win\": 0.0,\n        \"avg_loss_pct\": 0.0, \"avg_loss\": 0.0,\n        \"best_trade\": 0.0, \"worst_trade\": 0.0,\n        \"profit_factor\": 0.0, \"max_drawdown_pct\": 0.0, \"max_drawdown\": 0.0,\n        \"bars_used\": len(history),\n        \"result_label\": \"NO TRADES TRIGGERED\", \"enough_data\": False,\n    }\n\n    if len(history) < min_history:\n        return empty_result\n\n    trades     = []\n    in_trade   = False\n    entry_idx  = 0\n    entry_price= 0.0\n\n    i = min_history  # start after we have enough window for a score\n    while i < len(history):\n        if not in_trade:\n            window = history[max(0, i - min_history): i]\n            score, signal = _bt_score(window)\n\n            if score >= buy_threshold:\n                # Enter trade\n                in_trade    = True\n                entry_idx   = i\n                entry_price = history[i]\n\n        else:\n            # Already in trade \u2014 check exit each candle\n            result = simulate_trade(\n                entry_price,\n                [history[i]],      # one candle at a time\n                target_pct,\n                stop_pct\n            )\n            if result[\"outcome\"] in (\"WIN\", \"LOSS\"):\n                trades.append(result)\n                in_trade = False\n                # Jump past this trade\n                i += result[\"candles_held\"]\n                continue\n\n        i += 1\n\n    # Force-close any open position at last candle\n    if in_trade and entry_idx < len(history) - 1:\n        remaining = history[entry_idx + 1:]\n        result = simulate_trade(entry_price, remaining, target_pct, stop_pct)\n        trades.append(result)\n\n    # \u2500\u2500 Compute metrics \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n    total  = len(trades)\n    if total == 0:\n        return {**empty_result, \"candles\": len(history), \"enough_data\": True}\n\n    wins       = [t for t in trades if t[\"outcome\"] == \"WIN\"]\n    losses     = [t for t in trades if t[\"outcome\"] == \"LOSS\"]\n    win_rate   = round(len(wins) / total * 100, 1)\n\n    total_pnl  = sum(t[\"pnl_pct\"] for t in trades)\n    avg_win    = round(sum(t[\"pnl_pct\"] for t in wins)   / len(wins)   if wins   else 0.0, 2)\n    avg_loss   = round(sum(t[\"pnl_pct\"] for t in losses) / len(losses) if losses else 0.0, 2)\n\n    gross_profit = sum(t[\"pnl_pct\"] for t in wins)\n    gross_loss   = abs(sum(t[\"pnl_pct\"] for t in losses))\n    profit_factor = round(gross_profit / gross_loss if gross_loss > 0 else float(\"inf\"), 2)\n\n    # Max drawdown: largest peak-to-trough in cumulative PnL series\n    cum_pnl = 0.0\n    peak    = 0.0\n    max_dd  = 0.0\n    for t in trades:\n        cum_pnl += t[\"pnl_pct\"]\n        if cum_pnl > peak:\n            peak = cum_pnl\n        dd = peak - cum_pnl\n        if dd > max_dd:\n            max_dd = dd\n\n    if total_pnl > 0 and len(wins) > len(losses):\n        result_label = \"PROFITABLE \u2705\"\n    elif total_pnl > 0:\n        result_label = \"MARGINAL \ud83d\udfe1\"\n    else:\n        result_label = \"UNPROFITABLE \ud83d\udd34\"\n\n    return {\n        \"coin\":             coin,\n        \"candles\":          len(history),\n        \"trades\":           total,   # canonical key\n        \"total\":            total,   # alias \u2014 consumed by backtest_full / report\n        \"wins\":             len(wins),\n        \"losses\":           len(losses),\n        \"win_rate\":         win_rate,\n        \"total_pnl_pct\":    round(total_pnl, 2),\n        \"net_pnl\":          round(total_pnl, 2),   # alias used by backtest_full\n        \"avg_win_pct\":      avg_win,\n        \"avg_win\":          avg_win,                # alias used by report\n        \"avg_loss_pct\":     avg_loss,\n        \"avg_loss\":         avg_loss,               # alias used by report\n        \"profit_factor\":    profit_factor,\n        \"max_drawdown_pct\": round(max_dd, 2),\n        \"max_drawdown\":     round(max_dd, 2),       # alias used by report\n        \"bars_used\":        len(history),           # alias used by report\n        \"enough_data\":      True,\n    }\n\n\ndef legacy_backtest_report_v1(result):\n    \"\"\"\n    Format a backtest_coin() result dict into a Telegram message string.\n    Handles insufficient-data and zero-trade cases gracefully.\n    \"\"\"\n    coin = result[\"coin\"]\n\n    if not result[\"enough_data\"]:\n        return (\n            f\"\ud83d\udcc8 BACKTEST REPORT\\n\\n\"\n            f\"Coin: {coin}\\n\\n\"\n            f\"\u23f3 Not enough price history\\n\"\n            f\"Candles available: {result['candles']}\\n\"\n            f\"Minimum required:  {BACKTEST_MIN_HISTORY}\\n\\n\"\n            f\"The bot collects one candle every 30 seconds.\\n\"\n            f\"Run /backtest again after the background loop\\n\"\n            f\"has gathered more data.\"\n        )\n\n    if result[\"trades\"] == 0:\n        return (\n            f\"\ud83d\udcc8 BACKTEST REPORT\\n\\n\"\n            f\"Coin: {coin}\\n\"\n            f\"Candles: {result['candles']}\\n\\n\"\n            f\"No BUY signals triggered in this history window.\\n\"\n            f\"Score threshold: 70/100 \u2014 market may have been sideways.\"\n        )\n\n    pf_str = (\n        str(result[\"profit_factor\"])\n        if result[\"profit_factor\"] != float(\"inf\")\n        else \"\u221e (no losses)\"\n    )\n\n    pnl_sign = \"+\" if result[\"total_pnl_pct\"] >= 0 else \"\"\n\n    return (\n        f\"\ud83d\udcc8 BACKTEST REPORT\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Coin:     {coin}\\n\"\n        f\"Candles:  {result['candles']}\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Trades:   {result['trades']}\\n\"\n        f\"Wins:     {result['wins']}\\n\"\n        f\"Losses:   {result['losses']}\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Win Rate:       {result['win_rate']}%\\n\"\n        f\"Profit Factor:  {pf_str}\\n\\n\"\n        f\"PnL:            {pnl_sign}{result['total_pnl_pct']}%\\n\"\n        f\"Avg Win:        +{result['avg_win_pct']}%\\n\"\n        f\"Avg Loss:       {result['avg_loss_pct']}%\\n\"\n        f\"Max Drawdown:   -{result['max_drawdown_pct']}%\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Result: {result['result_label']}\"\n    )\n\n\n# =========================================\n# /backtest COMMAND  (PASS 9B \u2014 replaces stub)\n# =========================================\n\nasync def backtest_v2(update, context):\n    \"\"\"\n    /backtest <coin>\n    Runs BacktestEngine V1 on stored price history for the given coin.\n    Completely isolated \u2014 live state is never read or written.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not context.args:\n        coins = \", \".join(f\"/{c.lower()}\" for c in PHASE5[\"coins\"])\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            f\"Usage: /backtest SOL\\n\\n\"\n            f\"Available coins:\\n{coins}\"\n        )\n        return\n\n    coin = context.args[0].upper()\n    await update.message.reply_text(f\"\u23f3 Running backtest for {coin}...\")\n\n    try:\n        result = backtest_coin(coin)\n        msg    = backtest_report(result)\n    except Exception as e:\n        _record_error(\"backtest_v2\", e)\n        msg = f\"\u274c Backtest error: {e}\"\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(msg)\n\n\n# =========================================\n# BACKTESTING ENGINE V1  (PASS 9B)\n# =========================================\n#\n# Simulation rules:\n#  - Operates ONLY on price_history[coin] (read-only)\n#  - No mutation of positions, virtual_balance, trade_log, trade_stats\n#  - Uses same entry/exit logic as the live bot:\n#      Entry:  short_avg > long_avg AND momentum > 3% (same as analyze_coin score \u2265 70)\n#      Target: +7% (PHASE5[\"trade\"][\"target_percent\"])\n#      Stop:   -3% (PHASE5[\"trade\"][\"stoploss_percent\"])\n#      Trailing: activates at +7%, exits on -5% from peak (TRAILING config)\n# -  Each trade uses a fixed 10% of a simulated \u20b910000 capital\n# \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n\n# CoinDCX maker/taker fee applied on both sides of each simulated trade.\nTRADE_FEE_PCT = 0.001   # 0.1% per leg  \u2192  0.2% round-trip cost\n\n\ndef simulate_phase5_trade(entry_price, prices_after_entry, apply_fee=False):\n    \"\"\"\n    Simulate a single trade starting at entry_price.\n    Walks forward through prices_after_entry tick-by-tick.\n\n    apply_fee=True: deduct TRADE_FEE_PCT on entry and exit (realistic mode).\n    apply_fee=False: zero-fee mode (in-session /backtest default).\n\n    Returns a dict:\n      {\n        \"exit_price\":  float,\n        \"pnl_pct\":     float,   # percent gain/loss after optional fees\n        \"exit_reason\": str,     # \"target\" | \"stoploss\" | \"trailing\" | \"end_of_data\"\n        \"ticks_held\":  int,\n      }\n\n    Reads PHASE5 and TRAILING from module scope (no live-state mutation).\n    \"\"\"\n    target_pct   = PHASE5[\"trade\"][\"target_percent\"]       # 0.07\n    stoploss_pct = PHASE5[\"trade\"][\"stoploss_percent\"]     # 0.03\n    trail_act    = TRAILING[\"activate\"]                     # 0.07\n    trail_dist   = TRAILING[\"distance\"]                    # 0.05\n\n    target_price = entry_price * (1 + target_pct)\n    stop_price   = entry_price * (1 - stoploss_pct)\n\n    peak              = entry_price\n    trailing_active   = False\n\n    for tick, price in enumerate(prices_after_entry, 1):\n\n        # Update peak for trailing\n        if price > peak:\n            peak = price\n\n        # Activate trailing stop\n        pct_from_entry = (price - entry_price) / entry_price\n        if pct_from_entry >= trail_act:\n            trailing_active = True\n\n        # Check trailing exit\n        if trailing_active:\n            drop_from_peak = (peak - price) / peak\n            if drop_from_peak >= trail_dist:\n                fee = (2 * TRADE_FEE_PCT * 100) if apply_fee else 0\n                return {\n                    \"exit_price\":  price,\n                    \"pnl_pct\":     round((price - entry_price) / entry_price * 100 - fee, 2),\n                    \"exit_reason\": \"trailing\",\n                    \"ticks_held\":  tick,\n                }\n\n        # Check hard target\n        if price >= target_price:\n            fee = (2 * TRADE_FEE_PCT * 100) if apply_fee else 0\n            return {\n                \"exit_price\":  price,\n                \"pnl_pct\":     round((price - entry_price) / entry_price * 100 - fee, 2),\n                \"exit_reason\": \"target\",\n                \"ticks_held\":  tick,\n            }\n\n        # Check hard stop\n        if price <= stop_price:\n            fee = (2 * TRADE_FEE_PCT * 100) if apply_fee else 0\n            return {\n                \"exit_price\":  price,\n                \"pnl_pct\":     round((price - entry_price) / entry_price * 100 - fee, 2),\n                \"exit_reason\": \"stoploss\",\n                \"ticks_held\":  tick,\n            }\n\n    # Position still open at end of available data\n    last_price = prices_after_entry[-1] if prices_after_entry else entry_price\n    fee = (2 * TRADE_FEE_PCT * 100) if apply_fee else 0\n    return {\n        \"exit_price\":  last_price,\n        \"pnl_pct\":     round((last_price - entry_price) / entry_price * 100 - fee, 2),\n        \"exit_reason\": \"end_of_data\",\n        \"ticks_held\":  len(prices_after_entry),\n    }\n\n\ndef calculate_profit_factor(trades):\n    \"\"\"\n    Profit factor = gross_profit / gross_loss.\n    Returns float (inf if no losses).\n    \"\"\"\n    gross_profit = sum(t[\"pnl_pct\"] for t in trades if t[\"pnl_pct\"] > 0)\n    gross_loss   = abs(sum(t[\"pnl_pct\"] for t in trades if t[\"pnl_pct\"] < 0))\n    if gross_loss == 0:\n        return float(\"inf\")\n    return round(gross_profit / gross_loss, 2)\n\n\ndef calculate_backtest_drawdown(equity_curve):\n    \"\"\"\n    Maximum drawdown from an equity curve (list of cumulative % returns).\n    Returns max drawdown as a negative percentage.\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(equity_curve) < 2:\n        return 0.0\n    peak = equity_curve[0]\n    max_dd = 0.0\n    for val in equity_curve:\n        if val > peak:\n            peak = val\n        dd = (val - peak) / abs(peak) * 100 if peak != 0 else 0\n        if dd < max_dd:\n            max_dd = dd\n    return round(max_dd, 2)\n\n\n# backtest_coin(coin, min_history=20) \u2014 superseded by the implementation\n# above (uses _bt_score / buy_threshold). Removed duplicate to ensure\n# the correct definition is active.  (PASS 17 fix)\n\n\ndef backtest_report(result):\n    \"\"\"\n    Format a backtest_coin() result dict into a Telegram message string.\n    Handles both success and error results.\n    \"\"\"\n    if \"error\" in result:\n        return f\"\u274c Backtest Error\\n\\n{result['error']}\"\n\n    pf_str = (\n        f\"{result['profit_factor']}\"\n        if result[\"profit_factor\"] != float(\"inf\")\n        else \"\u221e\"\n    )\n\n    # Verdict\n    profitable = result[\"net_pnl\"] > 0 and result[\"win_rate\"] >= 50\n    edge = result[\"profit_factor\"] >= 1.5 if result[\"profit_factor\"] != float(\"inf\") else True\n    if profitable and edge:\n        verdict = \"\ud83d\udfe2 PROFITABLE EDGE\"\n    elif profitable:\n        verdict = \"\ud83d\udfe1 MARGINALLY PROFITABLE\"\n    else:\n        verdict = \"\ud83d\udd34 NO EDGE DETECTED\"\n\n    # Exit breakdown\n    exits = result[\"exit_counts\"]\n    exit_str = \"  \".join(\n        f\"{k.title()}: {v}\" for k, v in sorted(exits.items())\n    )\n\n    return (\n        f\"\ud83d\udcc8 PHASE5 BACKTEST\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Coin:           {result['coin']}\\n\"\n        f\"Bars Used:      {result['bars_used']}\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Trades:         {result.get('total', result.get('trades', 0))}\\n\"\n        f\"Wins:           {result.get('wins', 0)}\\n\"\n        f\"Losses:         {result.get('losses', 0)}\\n\"\n        f\"Win Rate:       {result['win_rate']}%\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Profit Factor:  {pf_str}\\n\"\n        f\"Avg Win:        +{result['avg_win']}%\\n\"\n        f\"Avg Loss:       {result['avg_loss']}%\\n\"\n        f\"Net PnL:        {'+' if result['net_pnl'] >= 0 else ''}{result['net_pnl']}%\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Max Drawdown:   {result['max_drawdown']}%\\n\"\n        f\"Best Trade:     +{result['best_trade']}%\\n\"\n        f\"Worst Trade:    {result['worst_trade']}%\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Exit Breakdown:\\n  {exit_str}\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Result: {verdict}\"\n    )\n\n\n# =========================================\n# /backtest COMMAND  (PASS 9B \u2014 replaces simple version)\n# =========================================\n\nasync def backtest(update, context):\n    if len(context.args) == 0:\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"Usage:\\n/backtest SOL\\n/backtest BTC\\n/backtest ETH\\n/backtest ZEC\"\n        )\n        return\n\n    coin = context.args[0].upper()\n\n    # Falls back to USDT pair if INR unavailable.\n# Data stored in historical_price_data \u2014 never touches live price_history.\n# \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n\nCANDLE_URL    = \"https://public.coindcx.com/market_data/candles\"\nUSDT_INR_PAIR = \"USDTINR\"   # ticker API pair (not used for candles)\n\n# Separate store for downloaded historical candles.\n# Structure: { \"SOL\": [close1, close2, ...], ... }\nhistorical_price_data = {}\n\n\ndef _coindcx_pair(coin, quote=\"USDT\"):\n    \"\"\"Return CoinDCX candle API pair string, e.g. B-BTC_USDT.\n\n    The candle endpoint (public.coindcx.com/market_data/candles) uses\n    B-COIN_QUOTE format \u2014 WITH the B- prefix and underscore separator.\n    This is a different symbol namespace from the ticker API (which uses\n    BTCUSDT with no prefix/separator).\n\n    Always use USDT as the quote for candles \u2014 INR candle history is\n    not available on the CoinDCX public API.\n    \"\"\"\n    return f\"B-{coin.upper()}_{quote.upper()}\"\n\n\ndef _fetch_candles_raw(pair, interval=\"1m\", limit=500):\n    \"\"\"\n    Fetch candle data from CoinDCX public endpoint.\n    Returns list of close prices in chronological order.\n    Raises on network error or empty response.\n\n    CoinDCX candle API hard limit is 500 bars per call.\n    Passing limit > 500 returns HTTP 200 with an empty list [].\n    \"\"\"\n    # Enforce API hard limit \u2014 values above 500 silently return []\n    safe_limit = min(limit, 500)\n\n    params = {\"pair\": pair, \"interval\": interval, \"limit\": safe_limit}\n\n    # \u2500\u2500 Debug logging \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n    print(f\"[Candle] URL={CANDLE_URL}\")\n    print(f\"[Candle] params={params}\")\n    # \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n\n    resp = requests.get(CANDLE_URL, params=params, timeout=15)\n\n    # \u2500\u2500 Debug logging \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n    print(f\"[Candle] HTTP status={resp.status_code}\")\n    print(f\"[Candle] response[:500]={resp.text[:500]}\")\n    # \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n\n    resp.raise_for_status()\n    data = resp.json()\n\n    if not isinstance(data, list) or not data:\n        raise ValueError(\n            f\"No candle data for {pair} \"\n            f\"(interval={interval}, limit={safe_limit}) \u2014 \"\n            f\"response was: {resp.text[:200]}\"\n        )\n\n    closes = []\n    for candle in data:\n        if isinstance(candle, (list, tuple)) and len(candle) >= 5:\n            closes.append(float(candle[4]))          # index 4 = close\n        elif isinstance(candle, dict):\n            c = (candle.get(\"close\") or candle.get(\"c\")\n                 or candle.get(\"Close\") or candle.get(\"close_price\"))\n            if c is not None:\n                closes.append(float(c))\n\n    if not closes:\n        raise ValueError(\n            f\"Could not parse closes for {pair} \u2014 \"\n            f\"got {len(data)} candles but none had a recognised close field. \"\n            f\"Sample candle: {data[0] if data else 'none'}\"\n        )\n    return closes\n\n\ndef fetch_historical_data(coin, days=7, interval=\"1m\"):\n    \"\"\"\n    Download historical candle closes for coin into historical_price_data[coin].\n\n    Attempt order (most \u2192 least data available on CoinDCX):\n      1. USDT pair, 1m candles (best availability)\n      2. USDT pair, 1h candles (fallback if 1m returns empty)\n      3. INR pair,  1m candles (sparse \u2014 last resort)\n\n    CoinDCX candle API hard limit: 500 bars per call.\n    Returns: {\"coin\", \"bars\", \"source\"} on success or {\"error\": str} on failure.\n    Never reads or writes live price_history.\n    \"\"\"\n    # 500 is the CoinDCX candle API hard limit.\n    # Requesting more returns HTTP 200 with an empty list [].\n    limit_1m = min(days * 24 * 60, 500)   # 1-min candles, max 500\n    limit_1h = min(days * 24,       500)   # 1-hour candles, max 500\n\n    errors = []\n\n    # \u2500\u2500 Attempt 1: USDT pair, 1m \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n    usdt_pair = _coindcx_pair(coin, \"USDT\")\n    actual_interval = interval\n    try:\n        usdt_closes = _fetch_candles_raw(usdt_pair, interval=interval, limit=limit_1m)\n        usdt_inr = _get_usdt_inr_rate()\n        closes = [round(p * usdt_inr, 2) for p in usdt_closes]\n        historical_price_data[coin.upper()] = closes\n        print(f\"[HistData] {coin} \u2014 {len(closes)} bars via {usdt_pair}\u00d7INR ({interval})\")\n        return {\"coin\": coin.upper(), \"bars\": len(closes),\n                \"source\": f\"{usdt_pair}\u00d7INR\", \"interval\": interval}\n    except Exception as e1:\n        errors.append(f\"USDT-{interval}: {e1}\")\n        print(f\"[HistData] {usdt_pair} {interval} failed: {e1}\")\n\n    # \u2500\u2500 Attempt 2: USDT pair, 1h (more history, less granularity) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n    if interval == \"1m\":\n        try:\n            usdt_closes = _fetch_candles_raw(usdt_pair, interval=\"1h\", limit=limit_1h)\n            usdt_inr = _get_usdt_inr_rate()\n            closes = [round(p * usdt_inr, 2) for p in usdt_closes]\n            historical_price_data[coin.upper()] = closes\n            print(f\"[HistData] {coin} \u2014 {len(closes)} bars via {usdt_pair}\u00d7INR (1h fallback)\")\n            return {\"coin\": coin.upper(), \"bars\": len(closes),\n                    \"source\": f\"{usdt_pair}\u00d7INR\", \"interval\": \"1h\"}\n        except Exception as e2:\n            errors.append(f\"USDT-1h: {e2}\")\n            print(f\"[HistData] {usdt_pair} 1h failed: {e2}\")\n\n    # INR candle pairs (BTCINR etc.) are not available on CoinDCX public\n    # candle endpoint \u2014 only USDT pairs have historical data.\n    # If both USDT attempts above failed, report the error.\n    msg = \" | \".join(errors)\n    print(f\"[HistData] All USDT attempts failed for {coin}: {msg}\")\n    return {\"error\": msg}\n\n\ndef _get_usdt_inr_rate():\n    \"\"\"Return live USDT/INR rate.\n\n    Priority:\n      1. market_state[\"usdt_inr\"] \u2014 set by update_market_cache() every 30 s\n      2. Hard fallback 86.0 \u2014 avoids making a candle API call for a reference rate\n         (INR candle pairs are not available on CoinDCX public candle endpoint)\n    \"\"\"\n    rate = market_state.get(\"usdt_inr\", 0)\n    if rate and rate > 0:\n        return rate\n    return 86.0   # safe fallback; market cache populates this within 30 s\n\n\ndef backtest_full(coin, days=7, interval=\"1m\", apply_fee=True):\n    \"\"\"\n    Fetch historical candles then run the Phase 5 backtest engine.\n    Uses historical_price_data \u2014 isolated from live price_history.\n\n    Temporarily swaps price_history[coin] with historical data so the\n    existing backtest_coin() function can be reused without duplication.\n    The original value is always restored in a finally block.\n    \"\"\"\n    fetch_result = fetch_historical_data(coin, days=days, interval=interval)\n    if \"error\" in fetch_result:\n        return {\"error\": f\"Data load failed: {fetch_result['error']}\"}\n\n    hist = historical_price_data.get(coin.upper(), [])\n    if len(hist) < BACKTEST_MIN_HISTORY:\n        return {\"error\": f\"Only {len(hist)} bars \u2014 need {BACKTEST_MIN_HISTORY}\"}\n\n    original = price_history.get(coin.upper())\n    try:\n        price_history[coin.upper()] = hist\n        result = backtest_coin(coin.upper())\n    finally:\n        # Always restore original live data \u2014 even if backtest_coin raises\n        if original is None:\n            price_history.pop(coin.upper(), None)\n        else:\n            price_history[coin.upper()] = original\n\n    if \"error\" in result:\n        return result\n\n    result[\"days_requested\"] = days\n    result[\"interval\"]       = interval\n    result[\"fee_applied\"]    = apply_fee\n    result[\"fee_pct\"]        = round(TRADE_FEE_PCT * 100, 3) if apply_fee else 0\n    result[\"data_source\"]    = fetch_result.get(\"source\", \"CoinDCX\")\n\n    if apply_fee and result.get(\"total\", 0) > 0:\n        fee_per_trade = 2 * TRADE_FEE_PCT * 100   # 0.2% round-trip\n        t = result[\"total\"]\n        result[\"net_pnl\"]    = round(result.get(\"net_pnl\", result.get(\"total_pnl_pct\", 0)) - fee_per_trade * t, 2)\n        result[\"avg_win\"]    = round(result.get(\"avg_win\",  result.get(\"avg_win_pct\",  0)) - fee_per_trade, 2)\n        result[\"avg_loss\"]   = round(result.get(\"avg_loss\", result.get(\"avg_loss_pct\", 0)) - fee_per_trade, 2)\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef backtest_full_report(result, coin, days):\n    \"\"\"Format backtest_full() result as a Telegram message.\"\"\"\n    if \"error\" in result:\n        return f\"\u274c Full Backtest Error\\n\\n{coin}\\n{result['error']}\"\n\n    pf     = result.get(\"profit_factor\", 0)\n    pf_str = str(pf) if pf != float(\"inf\") else \"\u221e\"\n    net    = result.get(\"net_pnl\", result.get(\"total_pnl_pct\", 0))\n    net_str = f\"+{net}%\" if net >= 0 else f\"{net}%\"\n\n    label = result.get(\"result_label\", \"\")\n    if not label:\n        label = (\"\ud83d\udfe2 PROFITABLE EDGE\" if net > 0 and pf >= 1.5\n                 else \"\ud83d\udfe1 MARGINALLY PROFITABLE\" if net > 0\n                 else \"\ud83d\udd34 NO EDGE DETECTED\")\n\n    fee_line = (f\"Fee Applied:    {result.get('fee_pct',0)}% per leg\\n\"\n                if result.get(\"fee_applied\") else \"\")\n\n    wr  = result.get(\"win_rate\", 0)\n    dd  = result.get(\"max_drawdown_pct\", result.get(\"max_drawdown\", 0))\n    best = result.get(\"best_trade\", 0)\n    worst = result.get(\"worst_trade\", 0)\n    aw  = result.get(\"avg_win\",  result.get(\"avg_win_pct\",  0))\n    al  = result.get(\"avg_loss\", result.get(\"avg_loss_pct\", 0))\n    bars = result.get(\"bars_used\", result.get(\"candles\", 0))\n\n    return (\n        f\"\ud83d\udcc8 PHASE5 FULL BACKTEST\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Coin:           {coin}\\n\"\n        f\"Period:         {days} day(s) of {result.get('interval','1m')} candles\\n\"\n        f\"Bars Used:      {bars}\\n\"\n        f\"Source:         {result.get('data_source','CoinDCX')}\\n\"\n        f\"{fee_line}\\n\"\n        f\"Trades:         {result.get('total', result.get('trades', 0))}\\n\"\n        f\"Wins:           {result.get('wins', 0)}\\n\"\n        f\"Losses:         {result.get('losses', 0)}\\n\"\n        f\"Win Rate:       {wr}%\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Profit Factor:  {pf_str}\\n\"\n        f\"Avg Win:        +{aw}%\\n\"\n        f\"Avg Loss:       {al}%\\n\"\n        f\"Net PnL:        {net_str}\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Max Drawdown:   -{dd}%\\n\"\n        f\"Best Trade:     +{best}%\\n\"\n        f\"Worst Trade:    {worst}%\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Result: {label}\"\n    )\n\n\n# =========================================\n# /backtestfull COMMAND  (PASS 10)\n# =========================================\n\nasync def backtestfull_cmd(update, context):\n    \"\"\"\n    /backtestfull SOL        \u2014 7-day default, 1m candles, 0.1% fees\n    /backtestfull BTC 3      \u2014 3-day\n    /backtestfull ETH 14     \u2014 14-day (max 30)\n    \"\"\"\n    if not context.args:\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"Usage:\\n\"\n            \"/backtestfull SOL\\n\"\n            \"/backtestfull BTC 3\\n\"\n            \"/backtestfull ETH 14\\n\\n\"\n            \"Downloads real CoinDCX candles,\\n\"\n            \"runs Phase 5 simulation with 0.1% fees.\"\n        )\n        return\n\n    coin = context.args[0].upper()\n    days = 7\n    if len(context.args) >= 2:\n        try:\n            days = max(1, min(int(context.args[1]), 30))\n        except ValueError:\n            await update.message.reply_text(\"Days must be a number between 1 and 30.\")\n            return\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n        f\"\u23f3 Fetching {days} day(s) of {coin} candles from CoinDCX...\\n\"\n        f\"Running Phase 5 simulation with 0.1% round-trip fees. Please wait.\"\n    )\n\n    result = backtest_full(coin, days=days, apply_fee=True)\n    msg    = backtest_full_report(result, coin, days)\n    await update.message.reply_text(msg)\n\n\n# =========================================\n# MARKET INTELLIGENCE ENGINE  (PASS 10)\n# =========================================\n\n# Live market context \u2014 updated by market_intelligence() each call.\n# Consumed by analyze_coin() to adjust confidence scores globally.\nmarket_context = {\n    \"regime\":              \"UNKNOWN\",   # BULL / BEAR / SIDEWAYS / HIGH_VOL\n    \"sentiment\":           \"NEUTRAL\",   # BULLISH / NEUTRAL / BEARISH\n    \"breadth_score\":       0,           # 0-100: % of coins trending up\n    \"volatility_level\":    \"NORMAL\",    # LOW / NORMAL / HIGH / EXTREME\n    \"confidence_modifier\": 0,           # added to every analyze_coin() score\n    \"heat_score\":          0,           # 0-100 aggregate market strength\n    \"coin_heat\":           {},          # coin \u2192 heat score (0-100)\n    \"last_updated\":        None,        # datetime string\n}\n\n\ndef market_intelligence():\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute a full market snapshot from price_history and market_state.\n    Populates market_context in-place and returns it.\n    Safe to call with empty or partial data.\n    \"\"\"\n    coins  = PHASE5[\"coins\"]\n    scores = []\n    moves  = []\n    heat   = {}\n\n    for coin in coins:\n        history = price_history.get(coin.upper(), [])\n        if len(history) < 5:\n            continue\n\n        short_avg = sum(history[-5:]) / 5\n        long_avg  = sum(history) / len(history)\n        move      = ((history[-1] - history[0]) / history[0] * 100\n                     if history[0] > 0 else 0)\n\n        # Coin score (mirrors analyze_coin logic)\n        s = 50\n        if short_avg > long_avg:\n            s += 20\n        if move > 3:\n            s += 15\n        if move > 7:\n            s += 10\n        s = min(s, 100)\n\n        scores.append(s)\n        moves.append(move)\n        heat[coin.upper()] = s\n\n    market_context[\"coin_heat\"]    = heat\n    market_context[\"last_updated\"] = datetime.now().strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\")\n\n    if not scores:\n        # Not enough data \u2014 keep defaults, zero modifier\n        market_context[\"confidence_modifier\"] = 0\n        market_context[\"regime\"]              = \"UNKNOWN\"\n        market_context[\"sentiment\"]           = \"NEUTRAL\"\n        market_context[\"breadth_score\"]       = 0\n        market_context[\"volatility_level\"]    = \"NORMAL\"\n        market_context[\"heat_score\"]          = 0\n        return market_context\n\n    avg_score    = sum(scores) / len(scores)\n    avg_move     = sum(moves)  / len(moves)\n    bullish_pct  = sum(1 for s in scores if s >= 70) / len(scores) * 100\n    breadth      = round(bullish_pct, 1)\n\n    # Volatility: std-dev of moves\n    mean_m  = avg_move\n    std_m   = (sum((m - mean_m) ** 2 for m in moves) / len(moves)) ** 0.5\n    if std_m > 8:\n        vol_level = \"EXTREME\"\n    elif std_m > 4:\n        vol_level = \"HIGH\"\n    elif std_m > 1.5:\n        vol_level = \"NORMAL\"\n    else:\n        vol_level = \"LOW\"\n\n    # Market regime\n    if avg_move > 4 and breadth >= 60:\n        regime = \"BULL\"\n    elif avg_move < -4 and breadth <= 30:\n        regime = \"BEAR\"\n    elif vol_level in (\"HIGH\", \"EXTREME\"):\n        regime = \"HIGH_VOL\"\n    else:\n        regime = \"SIDEWAYS\"\n\n    # Sentiment\n    if breadth >= 65:\n        sentiment = \"BULLISH\"\n    elif breadth <= 35:\n        sentiment = \"BEARISH\"\n    else:\n        sentiment = \"NEUTRAL\"\n\n    # Confidence modifier\n    modifiers = {\n        \"BULL\":     +10,\n        \"BEAR\":     -15,\n        \"HIGH_VOL\": -8,\n        \"SIDEWAYS\": -5,\n    }\n    conf_mod = modifiers.get(regime, 0)\n\n    # Heat score: weighted average of coin scores\n    heat_score = round(avg_score, 1)\n\n    market_context[\"regime\"]              = regime\n    market_context[\"sentiment\"]           = sentiment\n    market_context[\"breadth_score\"]       = breadth\n    market_context[\"volatility_level\"]    = vol_level\n    market_context[\"confidence_modifier\"] = conf_mod\n    market_context[\"heat_score\"]          = heat_score\n\n    return market_context\n\n\ndef market_sentiment():\n    \"\"\"\n    Return sentiment dict with label and confidence percentage.\n    \"\"\"\n    mc      = market_intelligence()\n    breadth = mc[\"breadth_score\"]\n\n    if mc[\"sentiment\"] == \"BULLISH\":\n        confidence = round(min(breadth, 100), 1)\n    elif mc[\"sentiment\"] == \"BEARISH\":\n        confidence = round(min(100 - breadth, 100), 1)\n    else:\n        # Neutral: closer to 50 \u2192 higher neutral confidence\n        confidence = round(100 - abs(breadth - 50) * 2, 1)\n\n    return {\n        \"label\":      mc[\"sentiment\"],\n        \"confidence\": confidence,\n        \"regime\":     mc[\"regime\"],\n        \"breadth\":    breadth,\n    }\n\n\ndef market_regime():\n    \"\"\"Return current market regime string.\"\"\"\n    return market_intelligence()[\"regime\"]\n\n\ndef market_heatmap():\n    \"\"\"\n    Return ordered list of (coin, score, label) for all watchlist coins.\n    \"\"\"\n    mc = market_intelligence()\n    rows = []\n    for coin in PHASE5[\"coins\"]:\n        s = mc[\"coin_heat\"].get(coin.upper(), 0)\n        if s >= 75:\n            label = \"\ud83d\udfe2 Strong\"\n        elif s >= 60:\n            label = \"\ud83d\udfe1 Neutral\"\n        else:\n            label = \"\ud83d\udd34 Weak\"\n        rows.append((coin.upper(), s, label))\n\n    # Sort: strongest first\n    rows.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n    return rows\n\n\n# =========================================\n# /market COMMAND  (PASS 10 \u2014 market-wide upgrade)\n# =========================================\n\nasync def market_cmd(update, context):\n    mc = market_intelligence()\n\n    regime_icons = {\n        \"BULL\":     \"\ud83d\udc02 Bull Market\",\n        \"BEAR\":     \"\ud83d\udc3b Bear Market\",\n        \"SIDEWAYS\": \"\u27a1\ufe0f  Sideways Market\",\n        \"HIGH_VOL\": \"\u26a1 High Volatility Market\",\n        \"UNKNOWN\":  \"\u2753 Insufficient Data\",\n    }\n    regime_str = regime_icons.get(mc[\"regime\"], mc[\"regime\"])\n\n    sent_icons = {\"BULLISH\": \"\ud83d\udfe2\", \"BEARISH\": \"\ud83d\udd34\", \"NEUTRAL\": \"\ud83d\udfe1\"}\n    sent_icon  = sent_icons.get(mc[\"sentiment\"], \"\u26aa\")\n\n    mod        = mc[\"confidence_modifier\"]\n    mod_str    = f\"+{mod}\" if mod > 0 else str(mod)\n\n    msg = (\n        f\"\ud83c\udf10 MARKET INTELLIGENCE\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Regime:      {regime_str}\\n\"\n        f\"Sentiment:   {sent_icon} {mc['sentiment']}\\n\"\n        f\"Breadth:     {mc['breadth_score']}% coins bullish\\n\"\n        f\"Volatility:  {mc['volatility_level']}\\n\"\n        f\"Heat Score:  {mc['heat_score']}/100\\n\\n\"\n        f\"AI Modifier: {mod_str} confidence pts\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Updated:     {mc['last_updated'] or 'not yet'}\"\n    )\n    await update.message.reply_text(msg)\n\n\n# =========================================\n# /sentiment COMMAND  (PASS 10)\n# =========================================\n\nasync def sentiment_cmd(update, context):\n    s = market_sentiment()\n\n    icons = {\"BULLISH\": \"\ud83d\udfe2\", \"BEARISH\": \"\ud83d\udd34\", \"NEUTRAL\": \"\ud83d\udfe1\"}\n    icon  = icons.get(s[\"label\"], \"\u26aa\")\n\n    breadth = s[\"breadth\"]\n    bull_pct = round(breadth, 1)\n    bear_pct = round(max(0, 100 - breadth - max(0, 100 - breadth - bull_pct)), 1)\n    neut_pct = round(max(0, 100 - bull_pct - bear_pct), 1)\n\n    msg = (\n        f\"\ud83d\udcad MARKET SENTIMENT\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Sentiment:  {icon} {s['label']}\\n\"\n        f\"Confidence: {s['confidence']}%\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Bullish Coins: {bull_pct}%\\n\"\n        f\"Neutral Coins: {neut_pct}%\\n\"\n        f\"Bearish Coins: {bear_pct}%\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Market Regime: {s['regime']}\"\n    )\n    await update.message.reply_text(msg)\n\n\n# =========================================\n# /heatmap COMMAND  (PASS 10)\n# =========================================\n\nasync def heatmap_cmd(update, context):\n    rows = market_heatmap()\n\n    if not rows:\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"\ud83d\udcca MARKET HEATMAP\\n\\n\"\n            \"\u23f3 Collecting price data...\\n\"\n            \"Try again in 1\u20132 minutes.\"\n        )\n        return\n\n    mc = market_intelligence()\n\n    lines = [f\"\ud83d\udcca MARKET HEATMAP\\n\"]\n    for coin, score, label in rows:\n        lines.append(f\"{coin:<6} {label}  ({score}/100)\")\n\n    lines.append(f\"\\nOverall: {mc['heat_score']}/100\")\n    lines.append(f\"Regime:  {mc['regime']}\")\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\"\\n\".join(lines))\n\n\n# =========================================\n# BACKTESTALL ENGINE  (PASS 11)\n# =========================================\n\ndef backtest_all_coins(days=7, apply_fee=True):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run backtest_full() across every coin in PHASE5[\"coins\"] sequentially.\n    Returns results sorted by profit_factor descending.\n    Error results placed at the bottom. No live state mutated.\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n    for coin in PHASE5[\"coins\"]:\n        result = backtest_full(coin.upper(), days=days, apply_fee=apply_fee)\n        result[\"coin_label\"] = coin.upper()\n        results.append(result)\n\n    ok  = [r for r in results if \"error\" not in r]\n    err = [r for r in results if \"error\" in r]\n\n    def _sort_key(r):\n        pf = r.get(\"profit_factor\", 0)\n        if pf == float(\"inf\"):\n            pf = 99.0\n        return (pf, r.get(\"net_pnl\", r.get(\"total_pnl_pct\", 0)))\n\n    ok.sort(key=_sort_key, reverse=True)\n    return ok + err\n\n\ndef backtest_all_summary(results, days):\n    \"\"\"\n    Format backtest_all_coins() output into a ranked Telegram message.\n    \"\"\"\n    ok  = [r for r in results if \"error\" not in r]\n    err = [r for r in results if \"error\" in r]\n\n    mc        = market_context\n    regime    = mc.get(\"regime\", \"UNKNOWN\")\n    sentiment = mc.get(\"sentiment\", \"NEUTRAL\")\n\n    regime_icons = {\n        \"BULL\": \"Bull\", \"BEAR\": \"Bear\",\n        \"SIDEWAYS\": \"Sideways\", \"HIGH_VOL\": \"High-Vol\", \"UNKNOWN\": \"Unknown\",\n    }\n    regime_str = regime_icons.get(regime, regime)\n\n    lines = [\n        f\"PHASE5 BACKTESTALL \u2014 {days}d\",\n        f\"Regime: {regime_str}  Sentiment: {sentiment}\",\n        \"\",\n        f\"{'Coin':<6} {'PF':>5} {'WR%':>6} {'PnL':>7} {'Trades':>6}\",\n        \"-\" * 34,\n    ]\n\n    for i, r in enumerate(ok, 1):\n        pf     = r.get(\"profit_factor\", 0)\n        pf_str = f\"{pf:.2f}\" if pf != float(\"inf\") else \"inf\"\n        wr     = r.get(\"win_rate\", 0)\n        net    = r.get(\"net_pnl\", r.get(\"total_pnl_pct\", 0))\n        net_s  = f\"+{net:.1f}%\" if net >= 0 else f\"{net:.1f}%\"\n        total  = r.get(\"total\", r.get(\"trades\", 0))\n        coin   = r[\"coin_label\"]\n        rank   = [\"1.\", \"2.\", \"3.\"][i - 1] if i <= 3 else f\"{i}.\"\n        lines.append(f\"{rank}{coin:<5} {pf_str:>5} {wr:>5.1f}% {net_s:>7} {total:>6}\")\n\n    if err:\n        lines.append(\"\")\n        lines.append(\"Failed:\")\n        for r in err:\n            lines.append(f\"  {r['coin_label']}: {str(r.get('error','?'))[:40]}\")\n\n    lines.append(\"-\" * 34)\n\n    if ok:\n        profitable = sum(1 for r in ok if r.get(\"net_pnl\", 0) > 0)\n        best       = ok[0]\n        pf_best    = best.get(\"profit_factor\", 0)\n        net_best   = best.get(\"net_pnl\", 0)\n\n        if profitable >= len(ok) * 0.6 and pf_best >= 1.5:\n            verdict = \"STRONG EDGE\"\n        elif profitable >= len(ok) * 0.4:\n            verdict = \"PARTIAL EDGE\"\n        else:\n            verdict = \"NO CLEAR EDGE\"\n\n        lines.append(f\"Best:    {best['coin_label']} (PF {pf_best:.2f}, {net_best:+.1f}%)\")\n        lines.append(f\"Verdict: {verdict}\")\n    else:\n        lines.append(\"All coins failed \u2014 no data available.\")\n\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n\n\n# =========================================\n# /backtestall COMMAND  (PASS 11)\n# =========================================\n\nasync def backtestall_cmd(update, context):\n    \"\"\"\n    /backtestall       \u2014 7-day sweep of all PHASE5 coins\n    /backtestall 3     \u2014 3-day sweep\n    /backtestall 14    \u2014 14-day sweep (max 30)\n    \"\"\"\n    days = 7\n    if context.args:\n        try:\n            days = max(1, min(int(context.args[0]), 30))\n        except ValueError:\n            await update.message.reply_text(\n                \"Usage:\\n/backtestall\\n/backtestall 3\\n/backtestall 14\\n\\n\"\n                \"Argument must be number of days (1-30).\"\n            )\n            return\n\n    coins    = PHASE5[\"coins\"]\n    est_secs = len(coins) * 3\n    await update.message.reply_text(\n        f\"Running Phase 5 backtest on {len(coins)} coins ({days}d)...\\n\"\n        f\"Fetching CoinDCX candles and simulating. ETA approx {est_secs}s.\"\n    )\n\n    results = backtest_all_coins(days=days, apply_fee=True)\n    msg     = backtest_all_summary(results, days)\n    await update.message.reply_text(msg)\n\n# =========================================\n# SCANNER SIGNAL BRIDGE (PASS 13)\n# =========================================\n\nsignal_queue = []\nsignal_history = []\n\n\ndef signal_threshold():\n    \"\"\"Configured scanner score threshold with backward-compatible default.\"\"\"\n    signals_cfg = PHASE5.setdefault(\"signals\", {})\n    return signals_cfg.setdefault(\"min_score\", 70)\n\n\ndef normalize_signal(signal):\n    \"\"\"Normalize scanner payload into the canonical bridge format.\"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(signal, dict):\n        return None\n\n    coin = str(signal.get(\"coin\", \"\")).upper().strip()\n    action = str(signal.get(\"action\", \"\")).upper().strip()\n\n    try:\n        score = float(signal.get(\"score\", 0))\n    except Exception:\n        score = 0\n\n    return {\n        \"coin\":coin,\n        \"action\":action,\n        \"score\":score,\n        \"source\":signal.get(\"source\", \"SCANNER\"),\n        \"timestamp\":signal.get(\"timestamp\", time.time()),\n        \"raw\":signal\n    }\n\n\ndef can_open_position(coin, score):\n    \"\"\"Bridge-local safety wrapper; preserves existing risk and cooldown systems.\"\"\"\n    if coin in positions:\n        return False, \"Position already open\"\n\n    cooldown, cooldown_reason = check_cooldown()\n    if cooldown:\n        return False, cooldown_reason\n\n    allowed, reason = risk_check(score)\n    if not allowed:\n        return False, reason\n\n    return True, \"Position allowed\"\n\n\ndef passes_market_intelligence_filter(coin):\n    \"\"\"Use PASS 10 market intelligence without mutating the market layer.\"\"\"\n    market = market_intelligence()\n    regime = market.get(\"regime\", \"UNKNOWN\")\n\n    if regime in [\"BEAR\", \"HIGH_VOL\"]:\n        return False, f\"Market regime blocked: {regime}\"\n\n    coin_heat = market.get(\"coin_heat\", {}).get(coin)\n    if coin_heat is not None and coin_heat < 50:\n        return False, f\"Coin heat too weak: {coin_heat}/100\"\n\n    return True, f\"Market filter passed: {regime}\"\n\n\ndef validate_signal(signal):\n    \"\"\"Validate scanner signal before paper execution.\"\"\"\n    normalized = normalize_signal(signal)\n\n    if normalized is None:\n        return False, \"Invalid signal payload\", None\n\n    coin = normalized[\"coin\"]\n    action = normalized[\"action\"]\n    score = normalized[\"score\"]\n\n    if action != \"BUY\":\n        return False, \"Only BUY signals are accepted\", normalized\n\n    if coin not in watchlist and coin not in PHASE5[\"coins\"]:\n        return False, f\"Coin not in watchlist: {coin}\", normalized\n\n    min_score = signal_threshold()\n    if score < min_score:\n        return False, f\"Score below threshold: {score} < {min_score}\", normalized\n\n    allowed, reason = can_open_position(coin, score)\n    if not allowed:\n        return False, reason, normalized\n\n    market_ok, market_reason = passes_market_intelligence_filter(coin)\n    if not market_ok:\n        return False, market_reason, normalized\n\n    risk_ok, risk_reason = risk_check(score)\n    if not risk_ok:\n        return False, risk_reason, normalized\n\n    return True, \"Signal accepted\", normalized\n\n\ndef paper_execute_signal(signal):\n    \"\"\"Execute accepted scanner signal as a paper buy.\"\"\"\n    global virtual_balance\n    global positions\n\n    coin = signal[\"coin\"]\n    score = signal[\"score\"]\n    price = get_cached_price_safe(coin)\n\n    if price <= 0:\n        return False, \"Price unavailable\"\n\n    allocation = get_allocation(score)\n    planned_amount = _safe_float(allocation.get(\"amount\", 0))\n    plan = create_grid_plan(coin, price, planned_amount)\n\n    if not plan:\n        return False, \"Grid plan unavailable\"\n\n    amount = _safe_float(plan[\"levels\"][0].get(\"allocation\"))\n\n    if amount <= 0:\n        return False, \"Allocation is zero\"\n\n    if amount > virtual_balance:\n        amount = virtual_balance\n\n    if amount <= 0:\n        return False, \"Insufficient paper balance\"\n\n    qty = amount / price\n    first_level = plan[\"levels\"][0]\n    first_level[\"filled\"] = True\n    first_level[\"filled_price\"] = price\n    first_level[\"filled_qty\"] = qty\n    first_level[\"filled_amount\"] = amount\n    first_level[\"filled_at\"] = time.time()\n    plan[\"filled_levels\"] = 1\n    plan[\"avg_cost\"] = price\n\n    virtual_balance -= amount\n\n    positions[coin] = {\n        \"buy_price\":price,\n        \"avg_cost\":price,\n        \"qty\":qty,\n        \"amount\":amount,\n        \"time\":time.time(),\n        \"peak\":price,\n        \"trailing_active\":False,\n        \"source\":signal.get(\"source\", \"SCANNER\"),\n        \"grid\":plan\n    }\n\n    log_trade(\n        coin,\n        \"BUY\",\n        price,\n        amount,\n        0\n    )\n    update_portfolio_history()\n    save_data()\n\n    return True, f\"Grid paper BUY L1 executed at Rs {round(price, 2)} for Rs {round(amount, 2)}\"\n\n\ndef process_signal(signal=None):\n    \"\"\"Validate and process one signal, defaulting to the oldest pending item.\"\"\"\n    if signal is None:\n        if not signal_queue:\n            return None\n        signal = signal_queue.pop(0)\n    else:\n        signal = normalize_signal(signal)\n\n    accepted, reason, normalized = validate_signal(signal)\n\n    record = normalized or {\n        \"coin\":\"UNKNOWN\",\n        \"action\":\"UNKNOWN\",\n        \"score\":0,\n        \"source\":\"UNKNOWN\",\n        \"timestamp\":time.time()\n    }\n\n    if accepted:\n        executed, execute_reason = paper_execute_signal(record)\n        record[\"result\"] = \"ACCEPTED\" if executed else \"REJECTED\"\n        record[\"reason\"] = execute_reason\n    else:\n        record[\"result\"] = \"REJECTED\"\n        record[\"reason\"] = reason\n\n    record[\"processed_at\"] = time.time()\n    signal_history.append(record)\n\n    if len(signal_history) > 200:\n        del signal_history[:-200]\n\n    return record\n\n\ndef receive_signal(signal):\n    \"\"\"Entry point for Scanner Bot -> Trading Bot signal bridge.\"\"\"\n    normalized = normalize_signal(signal)\n\n    if normalized is None:\n        record = {\n            \"coin\":\"UNKNOWN\",\n            \"action\":\"UNKNOWN\",\n            \"score\":0,\n            \"source\":\"UNKNOWN\",\n            \"timestamp\":time.time(),\n            \"result\":\"REJECTED\",\n            \"reason\":\"Invalid signal payload\"\n        }\n        signal_history.append(record)\n        return record\n\n    signal_queue.append(normalized)\n    return process_signal()\n\n\nasync def signals(update, context):\n    \"\"\"Show signal bridge queue and processing counters.\"\"\"\n    processed = len(signal_history)\n    accepted = len([s for s in signal_history if s.get(\"result\") == \"ACCEPTED\"])\n    rejected = len([s for s in signal_history if s.get(\"result\") == \"REJECTED\"])\n\n    msg = (\n        \"SCANNER SIGNAL BRIDGE\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Pending Signals: {len(signal_queue)}\\n\"\n        f\"Processed Signals: {processed}\\n\"\n        f\"Accepted Signals: {accepted}\\n\"\n        f\"Rejected Signals: {rejected}\\n\\n\"\n        f\"Threshold: {signal_threshold()}\"\n    )\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(msg)\n\n\nasync def signalhistory(update, context):\n    \"\"\"Show last 20 scanner bridge decisions.\"\"\"\n    if not signal_history:\n        await update.message.reply_text(\"No scanner signals processed yet.\")\n        return\n\n    lines = [\"SCANNER SIGNAL HISTORY\\n\"]\n    for item in signal_history[-20:]:\n        ts = datetime.fromtimestamp(item.get(\"processed_at\", item.get(\"timestamp\", time.time()))).strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\")\n        lines.append(\n            f\"{ts}\\n\"\n            f\"{item.get('coin')} {item.get('action')} score={item.get('score')}\\n\"\n            f\"Result: {item.get('result')}\\n\"\n            f\"Reason: {item.get('reason')}\\n\"\n        )\n\n    await update.message.reply_text(\"\\n\".join(lines))\n# =========================================\n# SCANNER INTEGRATION TESTING (PASS 14)\n# =========================================\n\ndef mock_scanner_signal():\n    \"\"\"Create a canonical test signal that mimics Scanner Bot output.\"\"\"\n    return {\n        \"coin\":\"SOL\",\n        \"action\":\"BUY\",\n        \"score\":85,\n        \"source\":\"TEST\",\n        \"timestamp\":time.time()\n    }\n\n\ndef signal_stats():\n    \"\"\"Return lightweight Scanner Bridge testing stats.\"\"\"\n    accepted = len([s for s in signal_history if s.get(\"result\") == \"ACCEPTED\"])\n    rejected = len([s for s in signal_history if s.get(\"result\") == \"REJECTED\"])\n    total = accepted + rejected\n    last_signal = signal_history[-1] if signal_history else None\n\n    return {\n        \"accepted\":accepted,\n        \"rejected\":rejected,\n        \"total\":total,\n        \"success_rate\":round((accepted / total) * 100, 2) if total else 0,\n        \"last_signal\":last_signal,\n        \"pending\":len(signal_queue)\n    }\n\n\ndef test_signal_pipeline():\n    \"\"\"Run mock Scanner -> Trading Bot -> paper execution path.\"\"\"\n    before_queue = len(signal_queue)\n    before_trades = len(trade_log)\n    before_history = len(signal_history)\n    before_portfolio = len(signal_history)\n    after_portfolio = len(portfolio_history)\n\n    trade_created = after_trades > before_trades\n    portfolio_updated = after_portfolio > before_portfolio\n    history_updated = after_history > before_history\n    entered_queue = after_queue > before_queue or history_updated\n\n    return {\n        \"signal\":signal,\n        \"result\":result,\n        \"entered_queue\":entered_queue,\n        \"processed\":result is not None,\n        \"trade_created\":trade_created,\n        \"trade_logged\":trade_created,\n equity_cmd(update, context):\n    \"\"\"Equity curve summary derived from portfolio_history.\"\"\"\n    if len(portfolio_history) < 2:\n        await update.message.reply_text(\n            \"\ud83d\udcc8 EQUITY CURVE\\n\\n\"\n            \"Not enough data yet.\\n\"\n            \"Portfolio history builds as the bot runs.\"\n        )\n        return\n\n    values  = [p[\"portfolio\"] first > 0 else 0.0\n    dd_pct      = round((peak - latest) / peak * 100, 2)   if peak  > 0 else 0.0\n    peak_pct    = round((peak  - first) / first * 100, 2)  if first > 0 else 0.0\n\n    direction
